  ---
  四、PlannerAgent 完整设计

  # src/haven/runtime/planner.py

  from __future__ import annotations

  import logging
  from typing import Any

  from langchain_core.language_models import BaseChatModel
  from langchain_core.messages import HumanMessage, SystemMessage

  from haven.runtime.runtime import AgentRuntime
  from haven.skills.registry import SkillRegistry
  from haven.skills.selector import SkillSelector, SkillSelectionResult

  logger = logging.getLogger("haven.planner")


  class PlannerAgent:
      """任务规划层。在 Runtime 之上，负责理解、拆解、规划。

      职责:
        1. 理解任务 — 意图分类 + 复杂度评估
        2. 拆解任务 — 将复杂任务分解为有序步骤
        3. 生成计划 — 输出结构化 ExecutionPlan
        4. 选择 Skill — 内嵌 LLM 选择（与规划合并为一次调用）
        5. 选择 Workflow — 匹配合适的预定义工作流

      与 Runtime 的关系:
        - Planner 不执行任务，只生成计划
        - Runtime 执行单个步骤（无状态的一次 LLM+Tool 调用）
        - WorkflowEngine 执行多步计划（有状态、有 checkpoint）

      用法::

          runtime = AgentRuntime()
          planner = PlannerAgent(runtime)
          # 一步完成规划+执行
          result = await planner.execute("帮我写一个爬虫")
          # 或分步：先看计划
          plan = await planner.plan("帮我写一个爬虫")
      """

      # ==================================================================
      # 初始化
      # ==================================================================

      def __init__(
          self,
          runtime: AgentRuntime,
          *,
          skill_registry: SkillRegistry | None = None,
          workflow_registry: WorkflowRegistry | None = None,  # 见下文
      ):
          self.runtime = runtime
          self.skill_registry = skill_registry or SkillRegistry
          self.workflow_registry = workflow_registry
          self._plan_cache: dict[str, ExecutionPlan] = {}

      @property
      def llm(self) -> BaseChatModel | None:
          return self.runtime.llm

      # ==================================================================
      # 公开 API
      # ==================================================================

      async def plan(self, task: str) -> ExecutionPlan:
          """分析任务并生成执行计划。

          不会修改 Runtime 状态。纯函数。
          """
          # Phase 0: 快速路径 — 简单对话跳过规划
          if self._is_trivial(task):
              return ExecutionPlan(
                  goal="日常对话",
                  intent="chat",
                  complexity="simple",
                  skills=[],
                  workflow=None,
                  steps=[],
                  reasoning="简单社交对话，无需规划",
              )

          # Phase 0.5: 缓存命中
          cache_key = self._cache_key(task)
          if cache_key in self._plan_cache:
              return self._plan_cache[cache_key]

          # Phase 1: LLM 规划（一次调用完成意图+拆解+skill选择+workflow匹配）
          plan = await self._llm_plan(task)

          # Phase 2: 依赖解析 — 补全 skill 依赖链
          plan.skills = SkillRegistry.resolve_dependencies(plan.skills)

          # Phase 3: 验证 — 检查 skill/workflow 是否存在
          plan = self._validate_plan(plan)

          # 写缓存
          self._plan_cache[cache_key] = plan
          if len(self._plan_cache) > 128:
              first = next(iter(self._plan_cache))
              del self._plan_cache[first]

          logger.info(
              "Plan: intent=%s complexity=%s skills=%s workflow=%s steps=%d",
              plan.intent, plan.complexity, plan.skills, plan.workflow,
  len(plan.steps),
          )
          return plan

      async def execute(self, task: str) -> str:
          """一步完成规划+执行。最常用的入口。"""
          plan = await self.plan(task)

          # 路径 1: 多步计划 + 有 workflow → WorkflowEngine
          if plan.workflow and self.workflow_registry:
              return await self._execute_workflow(plan, task)

          # 路径 2: 有步骤但无 workflow → Planner 自己编排 Runtime
          if plan.steps:
              return await self._execute_steps(plan, task)

          # 路径 3: 无步骤 (简单对话) → Runtime 直接用默认人格
          return await self.runtime.run(task)

      # ==================================================================
      # Phase 0: 快速路径
      # ==================================================================

      _TRIVIAL_PATTERNS: set[str] = {
          "你好", "hi", "hello", "谢谢", "thanks", "再见", "bye", "拜拜",
          "在吗", "你是谁", "你能做什么", "help", "帮助",
      }

      @classmethod
      def _is_trivial(cls, task: str) -> bool:
          cleaned = task.strip().lower().rstrip("?!。！？")
          return cleaned in cls._TRIVIAL_PATTERNS or len(cleaned) <= 2

      @staticmethod
      def _cache_key(task: str) -> str:
          import hashlib
          return hashlib.md5(task.encode()).hexdigest()

      # ==================================================================
      # Phase 1: LLM 规划
      # ==================================================================

      async def _llm_plan(self, task: str) -> ExecutionPlan:
          """一次 LLM 调用：意图分类 + 任务拆解 + skill 选择 + workflow 
  匹配。"""
          if self.llm is None:
              self.runtime._init_llm()

          system_prompt = self._build_planning_prompt()
          structured_llm = self.llm.with_structured_output(ExecutionPlan)

          try:
              plan: ExecutionPlan = await structured_llm.ainvoke([
                  SystemMessage(content=system_prompt),
                  HumanMessage(content=f"User request: {task}"),
              ])
          except Exception as exc:
              logger.warning("LLM planning failed: %s, returning empty plan",
  exc)
              return ExecutionPlan(
                  goal="",
                  intent="chat",
                  complexity="simple",
                  skills=[],
                  workflow=None,
                  steps=[],
                  reasoning=f"规划失败: {exc}",
              )

          return plan

      def _build_planning_prompt(self) -> str:
          """构建规划 system prompt。动态注入 skill 菜单 + workflow 菜单。"""
          skill_menu = SkillRegistry.get_selection_context()
          workflow_menu = self._get_workflow_menu()

          return PLANNER_SYSTEM_PROMPT.format(
              skill_menu=skill_menu,
              workflow_menu=workflow_menu,
          )

      def _get_workflow_menu(self) -> str:
          """获取可用工作流列表（供 LLM 选择）。"""
          if self.workflow_registry is None:
              return "(无可用工作流)"
          return self.workflow_registry.get_selection_context()

      # ==================================================================
      # Phase 3: 验证
      # ==================================================================

      def _validate_plan(self, plan: ExecutionPlan) -> ExecutionPlan:
          """验证并修正 plan 中的 skill/workflow 引用。"""
          available_skills = set(SkillRegistry.list_all().keys())

          # 过滤不存在的 skill
          valid_skills = [s for s in plan.skills if s in available_skills]
          invalid = set(plan.skills) - set(valid_skills)
          if invalid:
              logger.warning("Plan 引用了不存在的 skill: %s，已过滤", invalid)

          # 清理 steps 中不存在的 skill 引用
          for step in plan.steps:
              if step.skill and step.skill not in available_skills:
                  step.skill = None

          plan.skills = valid_skills
          return plan

      # ==================================================================
      # 执行路径 1: WorkflowEngine
      # ==================================================================

      async def _execute_workflow(self, plan: ExecutionPlan, task: str) -> str:
          """通过 WorkflowEngine 执行多步工作流。"""
          from haven.workflows.engine import WorkflowEngine

          engine = WorkflowEngine(self.runtime)
          return await engine.run(plan, task)

      # ==================================================================
      # 执行路径 2: Planner 自编排（无 workflow 但有 steps）
      # ==================================================================

      async def _execute_steps(self, plan: ExecutionPlan, task: str) -> str:
          """按步骤顺序执行，管理上下文传递。"""
          # 按拓扑序排序
          ordered = self._topological_sort(plan.steps)

          context: dict[str, str] = {}  # step.order → result
          results: list[str] = []

          for step in ordered:
              # 检查依赖是否完成
              for dep in step.depends_on:
                  if dep not in context:
                      logger.warning("步骤 %d 依赖步骤 %d 但后者未执行",
  step.order, dep)

              # 为当前步骤构建上下文
              dep_results = {
                  dep: context[dep]
                  for dep in step.depends_on
                  if dep in context
              }

              step_prompt = self._build_step_prompt(step, task, dep_results)

              # 激活 skill
              active_skills = [step.skill] if step.skill else []
              active_tools = self._tools_for_skills(active_skills)

              # 执行
              result = await self.runtime.run(
                  step_prompt,
                  active_skills=active_skills,
                  active_tools=active_tools,
              )

              context[step.order] = result
              results.append(result)

          return results[-1] if results else ""

      @staticmethod
      def _build_step_prompt(
          step: PlanStep,
          original_task: str,
          dep_results: dict[int, str],
      ) -> str:
          parts = [f"原始任务: {original_task}"]
          if dep_results:
              parts.append("前置步骤结果:")
              for order, result in dep_results.items():
                  parts.append(f"  步骤 {order}: {result[:500]}...")
          parts.append(f"当前步骤 ({step.order}): {step.description}")
          if step.expected_output:
              parts.append(f"预期产出: {step.expected_output}")
          return "\n".join(parts)

      @staticmethod
      def _tools_for_skills(skill_names: list[str]) -> list[str]:
          tools: set[str] = set()
          for name in skill_names:
              skill = SkillRegistry.get(name)
              if skill:
                  tools.update(skill.tools)
          return list(tools)

      @staticmethod
      def _topological_sort(steps: list[PlanStep]) -> list[PlanStep]:
          """按依赖关系拓扑排序。"""
          step_map = {s.order: s for s in steps}
          in_degree: dict[int, int] = {s.order: len(s.depends_on) for s in
  steps}
          adj: dict[int, list[int]] = {s.order: [] for s in steps}

          for s in steps:
              for dep in s.depends_on:
                  if dep in adj:
                      adj[dep].append(s.order)

          queue = [o for o, d in in_degree.items() if d == 0]
          result: list[PlanStep] = []

          while queue:
              order = queue.pop(0)
              result.append(step_map[order])
              for neighbor in adj.get(order, []):
                  in_degree[neighbor] -= 1
                  if in_degree[neighbor] == 0:
                      queue.append(neighbor)

          return result

  ---
  五、Planner System Prompt

  # 注入到 PlannerAgent._build_planning_prompt()

  PLANNER_SYSTEM_PROMPT = """\
  You are a task planner for an AI assistant named Haven ("健健").

  ## Your Job

  Analyze the user's request and produce a structured execution plan.

  ## Output Rules

  1. **goal**: Summarize the user's goal in ONE sentence (in the user's 
  language).
  2. **intent**: Classify into one of the available skill tags below.
     If no specific domain matches, use "chat".
  3. **complexity**:
     - "simple" — single-turn, one skill or no skill needed
     - "medium"  — 2-3 steps, one skill, sequential
     - "complex" — multi-step with dependencies, multiple skills
  4. **skills**: List skill names that are genuinely needed. Empty list for 
  casual chat.
  5. **workflow**: If a predefined workflow fits, use its name. Otherwise null.
  6. **steps**: Break the task into ordered steps. Each step specifies:
     - order: sequential number
     - description: what this step accomplishes (in the user's language)
     - skill: which skill to use (null if no specific skill)
     - depends_on: list of step orders that must finish first
     - expected_output: what this step should produce

  ## Important

  - For casual chat / simple questions, return: complexity="simple", skills=[], 
  steps=[].
  - Only select skills from the menu below. Do not invent skill names.
  - Only select workflows from the menu below. Do not invent workflow names.
  - For simple single-step tasks, use 1 step with the appropriate skill.
  - A step's skill must appear in the skills list.
  - Order skills by relevance, then by dependency.

  ## Available Skills

  {skill_menu}

  ## Available Workflows

  {workflow_menu}
  """

  ---
  六、WorkflowRegistry（配合 Planner）

  # src/haven/workflows/registry.py

  from haven.core.registry import Registry


  class WorkflowRegistry(Registry):
      """工作流注册表。存储预定义的工作流模板。"""

      _label = "Workflow"

      @classmethod
      def get_selection_context(cls) -> str:
          """格式化可用工作流列表（供 Planner LLM 选择）。"""
          if not cls._items:
              return "(无可用工作流)"

          lines: list[str] = []
          for name, wf in cls._items.items():
              lines.append(
                  f"### {name}\n"
                  f"- 描述: {getattr(wf, 'description', '无描述')}\n"
                  f"- 适用场景: {getattr(wf, 'use_cases', '通用')}\n"
                  f"- 步骤数: {getattr(wf, 'step_count', '?')}"
              )
          return "\n\n".join(lines)

  工作流注册方式：

  # src/haven/workflows/graphs/research.py

  from haven.workflows.registry import WorkflowRegistry


  @WorkflowRegistry.register("research_flow")
  class ResearchFlow:
      """调研工作流: 研究 → 总结。适用于需要搜集信息后汇总的任务。"""

      description = "深度调研工作流。Agent 先做广泛研究，再做结构化总结。"
      use_cases = "技术调研、市场分析、文献综述、竞品分析"
      step_count = 2

      async def run(self, runtime, plan, task): ...

  七、Planner + Runtime 集成方式

  7.1 完整调用链路

  User: "帮我写一个房价爬虫并做趋势分析"
          │
          ▼
  ┌─────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │ planner.execute(task)                    │
  │                                          │
  │  1. plan(task) → ExecutionPlan           │
  │     ┌──────────────────────────────┐     │
  │     │ LLM (1次调用)                 │     │
  │     │  intent: development          │     │
  │     │  skills: [coder, data_analysis]│    │
  │     │  workflow: research_flow      │     │
  │     │  steps: [S1, S2, S3, S4]      │     │
  │     └──────────────────────────────┘     │
  │                                          │
  │  2. SkillRegistry.resolve_dependencies() │
  │     skills → [coder, data_analysis]      │
  │                                          │
  │  3. workflow="research_flow" != null     │
  │     → _execute_workflow(plan, task)      │
  └────────────────┬────────────────────────┘
                   │
                   ▼
  ┌─────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │ WorkflowEngine.run(plan, task)           │
  │                                          │
  │  for step in plan.steps (拓扑序):        │
  │    ├─ runtime.activate_skills([step.skill])
  │    ├─ runtime.activate_tools(tools)      │
  │    ├─ runtime.run(step_prompt)           │
  │    │   ├─ build_system_prompt()          │
  │    │   │   └─ 人格 + 领域skill + 记忆     │
  │    │   ├─ build_messages()               │
  │    │   ├─ llm.ainvoke() + tool loop      │
  │    │   └─ return result                  │
  │    ├─ checkpoint(state)                  │
  │    └─ context[step.order] = result       │
  │                                          │
  │  return final_result                     │
  └─────────────────────────────────────────┘

          active_skills: Planner 选择的 skill。None → 只用 default skill。
          active_tools: Planner 选择的工具子集。None → 所有工具可用。

  7.3 AgentFactory 变更

  # src/haven/runtime/factory.py

  async def create_agent(...) -> PlannerAgent:
      """返回 PlannerAgent（而非 OrchestratorAgent 或 Runtime）。

      用户直接与 Planner 交互，Planner 内部持有 Runtime。
      """
      runtime = AgentRuntime()

      # 加载 skills
      _load_all_skills(runtime)

      # 加载工具
      _register_builtin_tools(runtime)

      # 加载 MCP
      if load_mcp:
          await _init_mcp_gateway(runtime)

      # 加载工作流
      workflow_registry = _init_workflow_registry()

      # 初始化 LLM（Runtime 持有）
      runtime._init_llm()

      # 装配 Planner
      planner = PlannerAgent(
          runtime,
          workflow_registry=workflow_registry,
      )

      # 设置会话
      runtime.memory.session_id = session_id
      runtime.memory.entity_name = entity_name
      runtime.memory.channel = channel
      runtime.state.session_id = session_id

      return planner

  7.4 CLI 调用变更

  # src/haven/cli/app.py — HavenApp._chat()

  async def _chat(self, user_input: str) -> None:
      # V1: response = await self.session.process(user_input)
      # V2:
      response = await self.planner.execute(user_input)   # 一行替代
      print_line(response)
      asyncio.create_task(self.planner.runtime.extract_facts_async())

  ---
  十、与之前设计的衔接

  本设计与前两次设计形成完整链路：

  第一次设计: Runtime + Skill 架构
    → AgentRuntime (LLM+Tool+Memory+State+Prompt)
    → 删除 4 个 specialist agent
    → 领域能力迁移到 .md skill

  第二次设计: LLM Skill Selection
    → SkillSelector (Phase 0→1→2→3)
    → 新 Skill Schema (tags/description/tools/dependencies)
    → SkillRegistry.resolve_dependencies()

  本次设计: PlannerAgent
    → 合并 SkillSelector 的 LLM 调用到规划 prompt 中 (1次调用)
    → 增加任务拆解 + 步骤依赖管理
    → 增加 Workflow 选择
    → PlannerAgent.execute() 作为用户唯一入口

  三者关系:
    User → PlannerAgent.plan(task)
             ├── LLM: 理解 + 拆解 + 选skill + 选workflow
             ├── SkillRegistry: resolve_dependencies()
             └── → ExecutionPlan
         → PlannerAgent.execute(task)
             ├── 路径A: WorkflowEngine.run(plan)     (复杂任务)
             ├── 路径B: self._execute_steps(plan)    (多步但无workflow)
             └── 路径C: Runtime.run(task)            (简单任务/对话)

✻ Worked for 2m 33s

❯ 目标：                                                                        
                                                                                
  将 workflows/                                                                 
                                                                                
  ResearchFlow                                                                  
  DevFlow                                                                       
  DiagnosisFlow                                                                 
                                                                                
  从串行流程升级为 Graph Workflow。                                             
                                                                                
  采用 LangGraph 思想。                                                         
                                                                                
  要求：                                                                        
                                                                                
  设计：                                                                        
                                                                                
  1. WorkflowState                                                              
  2. Node                                                                       
  3. Edge                                                                       
  4. Router                                                                     
  5. Retry                                                                      
  6. Checkpoint                                                                 
                                                                                
  示例：                                                                        
                                                                                
  Planner                                                                       
  ↓                                                                             
  Architect                                                                     
  ↓                                                                             
  Coder                                                                         
  ↓                                                                             
  Reviewer                                                                      
  ↓                                                                             
  Tester                                                                        
                                                                                
  失败：                                                                        
                                                                                
  Tester                                                                        
  ↓                                                                             
  Coder                                                                         
                                                                                
  形成循环。                                                                    
                                                                                
  输出：                                                                        
                                                                                
  1. Workflow 抽象层                                                            
  2. Graph 架构                                                                 
  3. 软件开发工作流示例                                                         
  4. 研究工作流示例                                                             
                                                                                
  不要修改代码。                                                                
                                                                                

● 以下是完整的 Graph Workflow 设计方案。

  ---
  Haven Workflow 升级：从串行到 Graph

  一、当前 vs 目标

  当前 (V1)                                 目标 (V2)
  ────────────                             ────────────

  ResearchFlow:                            WorkflowGraph (DAG + Router):
    researcher.run() ──→ coder.run()
                                           ┌──────────┐
  DevFlow:                                 │ Planner  │
    researcher.run() ──→ coder.run()       └────┬─────┘
                                                 │
  DiagnosisFlow:                            ┌────▼─────┐
    researcher.run() ──→ doctor.run()       │ Architect│
                                            └────┬─────┘
  问题:                                          │
  · 纯串行 A→B                             ┌────▼─────┐
  · 无分支/条件路由                         │  Coder   │◄──────┐
  · 无失败重试                              └────┬─────┘       │
  · 无状态恢复                                   │             │
  · 代码中硬编码                             ┌────▼─────┐       │
  · 无并行执行                              │ Reviewer │       │
                                            └────┬─────┘       │
                                                 │             │
                                            ┌────▼─────┐  ┌────┴─────┐
                                            │  Tester  │──│  Router  │
                                            └────┬─────┘  └──────────┘
                                                 │ pass         │ fail
                                            ┌────▼─────┐       │
                                            │   END    │       └──→ Coder
                                            └──────────┘       (重试最多3次)

  ---
  二、WorkflowState 设计

  2.1 基类

  # src/haven/workflows/state.py

  from __future__ import annotations

  from dataclasses import dataclass, field
  from typing import Any

  from langchain_core.messages import BaseMessage


  @dataclass
  class WorkflowState:
      """所有工作流的基类状态。在节点间流动并持久化。

      每个具体工作流继承此类，添加领域特定字段。
      """

      # ========== 任务标识 ==========
      task: str = ""                              # 用户原始请求
      session_id: str = "default"                 # 会话 ID

      # ========== 消息历史 ==========
      messages: list[BaseMessage] = field(default_factory=list)

      # ========== 执行控制 ==========
      current_node: str = ""                      # 当前所在节点名
      node_outputs: dict[str, str] = field(       # 每个节点的输出
          default_factory=dict
      )
      node_retry_counts: dict[str, int] = field(  # 每个节点的重试次数
          default_factory=dict
      )
      max_retries_per_node: int = 3               # 全局最大重试

      # ========== 执行结果 ==========
      status: str = "pending"                     # pending | running | 
  completed | failed
      final_output: str = ""                      # 最终结果
      errors: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)  # 错误日志

      # ========== 时间 ==========
      started_at: float = 0.0
      checkpoint_at: float = 0.0

      # ========== 不可序列化的运行时引用 ==========
      _runtime: Any = field(default=None, repr=False)

  2.2 软件开发工作流专用 State

  @dataclass
  class DevWorkflowState(WorkflowState):
      """软件开发工作流专用状态。"""

      # 架构阶段
      architecture_doc: str = ""               # 架构设计文档
      tech_stack: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)

      # 编码阶段
      source_code: str = ""                    # 生成的代码
      code_language: str = "python"            # 编程语言

      # Review 阶段
      review_feedback: str = ""                # 审查反馈
      review_score: float = 0.0                # 审查评分 (0-100)
      review_blockers: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)  # 阻塞项

      # Test 阶段
      test_report: str = ""                    # 测试报告
      test_passed: bool = False                # 测试是否通过
      test_failures: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)    # 失败项

      # 质量门禁
      quality_gate_passed: bool = False

  2.3 研究工作流专用 State

  @dataclass
  class ResearchWorkflowState(WorkflowState):
      """调研工作流专用状态。"""

      research_topic: str = ""                 # 调研主题
      search_queries: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)   # 搜索关键词
      raw_findings: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)     # 原始发现
      analyzed_insights: str = ""              # 分析后的洞察
      final_report: str = ""                   # 最终报告
      sources: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)          # 信息来源

  ---
  三、Node 设计

  3.1 Node 类型

  # src/haven/workflows/nodes.py

  from __future__ import annotations

  from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
  from typing import Any, Callable, Awaitable

  # Node 函数签名
  NodeFunc = Callable[[WorkflowState], Awaitable[dict[str, Any]]]
  # 返回 dict 用于部分更新 state


  class WorkflowNode(ABC):
      """工作流节点的抽象基类。

      每个节点 = 一个 Runtime 调用 + 一个特定 Skill。

      子类只需实现:
        - skill_name: 节点使用的 skill 名
        - _build_prompt(state): 根据 state 构建发给 Runtime 的 prompt
        - _process_output(output, state): 处理 Runtime 返回结果,更新 state
      """

      name: str = ""
      skill_name: str | None = None
      max_retries: int = 0          # 0 = 使用全局设定
      timeout_seconds: int = 300

      async def __call__(self, state: WorkflowState) -> dict[str, Any]:
          """节点执行入口。符合 LangGraph node 签名。"""
          runtime = state._runtime

          # 构建立即执行的 prompt
          prompt = self._build_prompt(state)

          # 调用 Runtime（单次 LLM+Tool 调用）
          active_skills = [self.skill_name] if self.skill_name else []
          active_tools = self._resolve_tools(state)

          try:
              output = await runtime.run(
                  prompt,
                  active_skills=active_skills,
                  active_tools=active_tools,
                  context=self._build_context(state),
              )
          except Exception as exc:
              return self._handle_error(state, exc)

          # 处理输出，返回 state 更新
          updates = self._process_output(output, state)
          updates["current_node"] = self.name
          updates["node_outputs"] = {
              **state.node_outputs,
              self.name: output,
          }
          return updates

      @abstractmethod
      def _build_prompt(self, state: WorkflowState) -> str:
          """根据 state 构建发给 Runtime 的 prompt。"""
          ...

      def _process_output(self, output: str, state: WorkflowState) -> dict[str, 
  Any]:
          """处理 LLM 输出，返回 state 更新字段。默认：不做特殊处理。"""
          return {}

      def _resolve_tools(self, state: WorkflowState) -> list[str]:
          """解析本节点需要的工具列表。默认从 skill 声明中获取。"""
          if self.skill_name:
              from haven.skills.registry import SkillRegistry
              skill = SkillRegistry.get(self.skill_name)
              if skill:
                  return skill.tools
          return []

      def _build_context(self, state: WorkflowState) -> dict[str, str]:
          """构建传给 Runtime 的前置步骤上下文。"""
          return {}

      def _handle_error(self, state: WorkflowState, exc: Exception) -> dict[str,
   Any]:
          """节点执行失败时的处理。"""
          return {
              "errors": [*state.errors, f"[{self.name}] {exc}"],
              "status": "failed",
              "current_node": self.name,
          }

  3.2 具体 Node 示例

  # ==================================================================
  # 软件开发工作流节点
  # ==================================================================

  class PlannerNode(WorkflowNode):
      name = "planner"
      skill_name = "coder"

      def _build_prompt(self, state: WorkflowState) -> str:
          return f"""\
  ## 任务：需求分析

  分析用户需求，明确要做的事情:

  用户需求: {state.task}

  输出:
  1. 需求概述（一句话）
  2. 功能清单（列表）
  3. 技术约束
  4. 验收标准
  """

      def _process_output(self, output: str, state: DevWorkflowState) -> dict:
          return {}  # planner 输出供 architect 参考

  # --------

  class ArchitectNode(WorkflowNode):
      name = "architect"
      skill_name = "coder"

      def _build_prompt(self, state: DevWorkflowState) -> str:
          plan = state.node_outputs.get("planner", "")
          return f"""\
  ## 任务：架构设计

  基于需求分析设计系统架构:

  原始需求: {state.task}

  需求分析:
  {plan}

  输出:
  1. 架构模式（如 MVC, 微服务, 单体）
  2. 技术栈选择
  3. 目录/模块结构
  4. 数据流设计
  5. 关键接口定义
  """

      def _process_output(self, output: str, state: DevWorkflowState) -> dict:
          return {"architecture_doc": output}

  # --------

  class CoderNode(WorkflowNode):
      name = "coder"
      skill_name = "coder"
      max_retries = 3    # Coder 可以重试 3 次

      def _build_prompt(self, state: DevWorkflowState) -> str:
          arch = state.architecture_doc
          review_feedback = state.node_outputs.get("reviewer", "")
          test_failures = state.test_failures

          parts = [
              f"## 任务：编写代码\n",
              f"需求: {state.task}",
              f"架构设计: {arch}",
          ]

          # 重试时注入上一次的反馈
          if state.node_retry_counts.get("coder", 0) > 0:
              parts.append("\n## 这是重新编码，以下是你上次的问题:")
              if review_feedback:
                  parts.append(f"\n### Review 反馈:\n{review_feedback}")
              if test_failures:
                  parts.append(f"\n### 测试失败:\n" + "\n".join(f"- {f}" for f
  in test_failures))
              parts.append("\n请修正以上所有问题后重新生成代码。")

          parts.append("\n输出: 完整的可运行代码，包含注释。")
          return "\n".join(parts)

      def _process_output(self, output: str, state: DevWorkflowState) -> dict:
          code = self._extract_code(output)
          return {"source_code": code}

      @staticmethod
      def _extract_code(text: str) -> str:
          """从 LLM 输出中提取代码块。"""
          if "```" in text:
              parts = text.split("```")
              # 取第一个代码块
              for i, part in enumerate(parts):
                  if i % 2 == 1:
                      # 去除语言标签
                      lines = part.split("\n")
                      if lines[0].strip() in ("python", "js", "go", "rust",
  "shell", "bash", "sql", "",):
                          return "\n".join(lines[1:])
                      return part
          return text

  # --------

  class ReviewerNode(WorkflowNode):
      name = "reviewer"
      skill_name = "code_review"

      def _build_prompt(self, state: DevWorkflowState) -> str:
          return f"""\
  ## 任务：代码审查

  审查以下代码:

  {state.source_code}

  原始需求: {state.task}

  按以下维度评分（0-100）:
  1. 功能正确性 (40分)
  2. 代码质量 (20分)
  3. 安全性 (20分)
  4. 性能 (10分)
  5. 可维护性 (10分)

  输出格式:
  - 总分: X/100
  - 通过: yes/no (>= 70 为通过)
  - 阻塞项: (列出必须修改的问题)
  - 建议项: (列出优化建议)
  """

      def _process_output(self, output: str, state: DevWorkflowState) -> dict:
          score = self._parse_score(output)
          blockers = self._parse_blockers(output)
          return {
              "review_feedback": output,
              "review_score": score,
              "review_blockers": blockers,
          }

      @staticmethod
      def _parse_score(text: str) -> float:
          import re
          match = re.search(r"总分[：:]\s*(\d+)/?100", text)
          return float(match.group(1)) if match else 0.0

      @staticmethod
      def _parse_blockers(text: str) -> list[str]:
          blockers: list[str] = []
          in_section = False
          for line in text.split("\n"):
              if "阻塞" in line or "blocker" in line.lower():
                  in_section = True
                  continue
              if in_section and line.strip().startswith("-"):
                  blockers.append(line.strip("- "))
              elif in_section and line.strip() == "":
                  break
          return blockers

  # --------

  class TesterNode(WorkflowNode):
      name = "tester"
      skill_name = "coder"

      def _build_prompt(self, state: DevWorkflowState) -> str:
          return f"""\
  ## 任务：测试验证

  编写测试用例并验证以下代码:

  {state.source_code}

  原始需求: {state.task}

  要求:
  1. 根据需求写出测试用例
  2. 执行测试（如果有 code_exec 工具）
  3. 验证边界条件和错误处理

  输出:
  - 通过: yes/no
  - 失败项: (列出不通过的用例)
  - 测试覆盖: (估算覆盖率)
  """

      def _process_output(self, output: str, state: DevWorkflowState) -> dict:
          passed = "通过: yes" in output or "通过：是" in output or "PASS" in
  output.upper()
          failures = self._parse_failures(output)
          return {
              "test_report": output,
              "test_passed": passed,
              "test_failures": failures,
          }

      @staticmethod
      def _parse_failures(text: str) -> list[str]:
          failures: list[str] = []
          in_section = False
          for line in text.split("\n"):
              if "失败" in line and ":" in line:
                  in_section = True
                  continue
              if in_section and line.strip().startswith("-"):
                  failures.append(line.strip("- "))
              elif in_section and line.strip() == "":
                  break
          return failures

  ---
  四、Edge 与 Router 设计

  4.1 Edge 类型

  # src/haven/workflows/edges.py

  from __future__ import annotations

  from enum import Enum, auto
  from typing import Any, Callable, Awaitable

  from haven.workflows.state import WorkflowState


  # ============================================================================
  # 无条件边
  # ============================================================================

  class Edge:
      """无条件边。A 完成后总是到 B。"""

      def __init__(self, source: str, target: str):
          self.source = source
          self.target = target

      def __repr__(self) -> str:
          return f"{self.source} → {self.target}"


  # ============================================================================
  # 条件路由
  # ============================================================================

  # Router 函数签名: (state) → str  (返回下一个节点名)
  RouterFunc = Callable[[WorkflowState], str | Awaitable[str]]


  class ConditionalEdge:
      """条件边。根据 Router 函数返回的目标节点名动态决定下一步。

      Router 返回:
        - 节点名 → 跳到该节点
        - "__END__" → 工作流结束
        - "__RETRY__" → 重试当前节点（检查 retry_count）
      """

      END = "__END__"
      RETRY = "__RETRY__"

      def __init__(
          self,
          source: str,
          router: RouterFunc,
          route_map: dict[str, str] | None = None,
      ):
          self.source = source
          self.router = router
          # route_map: router 的返回值 → 实际节点名（可选映射）
          self.route_map = route_map or {}

      async def resolve(self, state: WorkflowState) -> str:
          result = self.router(state)
          if hasattr(result, "__await__"):
              result = await result
          return self.route_map.get(result, result)

      def __repr__(self) -> str:
          return f"{self.source} → [router]"


  # ============================================================================
  # 内置 Router 函数
  # ============================================================================

  def review_router(state: WorkflowState) -> str:
      """Review 路由：评分 >= 70 → 进入 Test；< 70 → 返回 Coder。"""
      dev_state = state  # type: DevWorkflowState
      retries = dev_state.node_retry_counts.get("coder", 0)

      if dev_state.review_score >= 70 and not dev_state.review_blockers:
          return "tester"

      if retries >= dev_state.max_retries_per_node:
          return ConditionalEdge.END  # 超过重试上限，结束

      return "coder"


  def test_router(state: WorkflowState) -> str:
      """测试路由：通过 → 结束；失败 → 返回 Coder。"""
      dev_state = state  # type: DevWorkflowState
      retries = dev_state.node_retry_counts.get("coder", 0)

      if dev_state.test_passed:
          return ConditionalEdge.END

      if retries >= dev_state.max_retries_per_node:
          return ConditionalEdge.END  # 超限，以失败状态结束

      return "coder"


  def quality_gate_router(state: WorkflowState) -> str:
      """质量门禁：review >= 80 且 test 通过 → 结束；否则 → Coder 重试。"""
      dev_state = state  # type: DevWorkflowState
      retries = dev_state.node_retry_counts.get("coder", 0)

      if dev_state.review_score >= 80 and dev_state.test_passed:
          return ConditionalEdge.END

      if retries >= dev_state.max_retries_per_node:
          return "human_review"  # 升级到人工审查

      return "coder"

  ---
  五、WorkflowGraph 引擎

  # src/haven/workflows/graph.py

  from __future__ import annotations

  import time
  import logging
  from typing import Any

  from haven.workflows.state import WorkflowState
  from haven.workflows.nodes import NodeFunc
  from haven.workflows.edges import Edge, ConditionalEdge, RouterFunc

  logger = logging.getLogger("haven.workflow")


  class WorkflowGraph:
      """LangGraph 风格的 DAG 工作流引擎。

      用法::

          graph = WorkflowGraph(DevWorkflowState)
          graph.add_node("planner", PlannerNode())
          graph.add_node("architect", ArchitectNode())
          graph.add_node("coder", CoderNode())
          graph.add_node("reviewer", ReviewerNode())
          graph.add_node("tester", TesterNode())

          graph.add_edge("planner", "architect")
          graph.add_edge("architect", "coder")
          graph.add_edge("coder", "reviewer")
          graph.add_edge("reviewer", "tester")
          graph.add_conditional_edge("tester", test_router)
          graph.set_entry_point("planner")

          graph.set_checkpointer(SQLiteCheckpointer())

          result = await graph.run(DevWorkflowState(task="写一个排序算法"))
      """

      # Sentinel for graph termination
      END = "__END__"

      def __init__(self, state_cls: type[WorkflowState]):
          self._state_cls = state_cls
          self._nodes: dict[str, NodeFunc] = {}
          self._edges: dict[str, Edge | ConditionalEdge] = {}
          self._entry_point: str = ""
          self._checkpointer: Checkpointer | None = None
          self._max_graph_iterations: int = 20  # 防止死循环

      # ==================================================================
      # 图构建 API
      # ==================================================================

      def add_node(self, name: str, node: NodeFunc) -> "WorkflowGraph":
          """注册节点。node 可以是 WorkflowNode 实例或普通 async 函数。"""
          self._nodes[name] = node
          return self

      def add_edge(self, source: str, target: str) -> "WorkflowGraph":
          """无条件边：source 执行完 → target。"""
          if source in self._edges:
              raise ValueError(f"节点 '{source}' 已有一条出边。用
  add_conditional_edge 代替。")
          self._edges[source] = Edge(source, target)
          return self

      def add_conditional_edge(
          self, source: str, router: RouterFunc
      ) -> "WorkflowGraph":
          """条件边：source 执行完 → router(state) 决定下一步。"""
          if source in self._edges:
              raise ValueError(f"节点 '{source}' 已有一条出边。")
          self._edges[source] = ConditionalEdge(source, router)
          return self

      def set_entry_point(self, name: str) -> "WorkflowGraph":
          """设置入口节点。"""
          if name not in self._nodes:
              raise ValueError(f"入口节点 '{name}' 未注册")
          self._entry_point = name
          return self

      def set_checkpointer(self, checkpointer: "Checkpointer") ->
  "WorkflowGraph":
          """设置状态持久化后端。"""
          self._checkpointer = checkpointer
          return self

      # ==================================================================
      # 执行
      # ==================================================================

      async def run(
          self,
          state: WorkflowState,
          *,
          runtime: Any = None,           # AgentRuntime 实例
          resume_from: str | None = None,  # 从 checkpoint 恢复
      ) -> WorkflowState:
          """执行工作流图。"""

          # 恢复 checkpoint
          if resume_from and self._checkpointer:
              saved = await self._checkpointer.load(resume_from)
              if saved:
                  state = saved
                  logger.info("从 checkpoint 恢复: %s → %s", resume_from,
  state.current_node)

          state._runtime = runtime
          state.status = "running"
          state.started_at = time.time()

          current_node = resume_from or self._entry_point
          iteration = 0

          while iteration < self._max_graph_iterations:
              iteration += 1

              # 检查终止
              if current_node == self.END:
                  state.status = "completed"
                  state.final_output = self._build_final_output(state)
                  break

              # 获取节点
              node = self._nodes.get(current_node)
              if node is None:
                  state.errors.append(f"节点 '{current_node}' 未注册")
                  state.status = "failed"
                  break

              # 执行节点
              state.current_node = current_node
              logger.info("Workflow[%d]: executing '%s'", iteration,
  current_node)

              try:
                  updates = await node(state)
              except Exception as exc:
                  logger.error("Workflow: 节点 '%s' 异常: %s", current_node,
  exc)
                  updates = {
                      "errors": [*state.errors, f"[{current_node}] {exc}"],
                      "status": "failed",
                  }

              # 应用 state 更新
              self._apply_updates(state, updates)

              # 检查是否失败
              if state.status == "failed":
                  break

              # 更新重试计数
              self._update_retry_count(state, current_node)

              # Checkpoint
              if self._checkpointer:
                  await self._checkpointer.save(
                      state.session_id,
                      current_node,
                      state,
                  )

              # 确定下一个节点
              edge = self._edges.get(current_node)
              if edge is None:
                  # 叶节点：结束
                  state.status = "completed"
                  state.final_output = self._build_final_output(state)
                  break
              elif isinstance(edge, ConditionalEdge):
                  next_node = await edge.resolve(state)
              else:
                  next_node = edge.target

              # 处理 RETRY
              if next_node == ConditionalEdge.RETRY:
                  retries = state.node_retry_counts.get(current_node, 0)
                  if retries < state.max_retries_per_node:
                      next_node = current_node  # 重新执行当前节点
                      logger.info("Workflow: 重试节点 '%s' (第 %d 次)",
  current_node, retries + 1)
                  else:
                      state.status = "failed"
                      state.errors.append(f"节点 '{current_node}'
  超过最大重试次数 {state.max_retries_per_node}")
                      break

              current_node = next_node

          # 最终 checkpoint
          if self._checkpointer and state.status in ("completed", "failed"):
              await self._checkpointer.save(state.session_id, self.END, state)

          return state

      # ==================================================================
      # 内部
      # ==================================================================

      @staticmethod
      def _apply_updates(state: WorkflowState, updates: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
          """将 node 返回的 dict 更新到 state 上。"""
          for key, value in updates.items():
              if hasattr(state, key):
                  # 特殊处理 dict 字段：做 merge
                  current = getattr(state, key)
                  if isinstance(current, dict) and isinstance(value, dict):
                      current.update(value)
                  # 特殊处理 list 字段：做 extend
                  elif isinstance(current, list) and isinstance(value, list):
                      current.extend(value)
                  else:
                      setattr(state, key, value)

      @staticmethod
      def _update_retry_count(state: WorkflowState, node_name: str) -> None:
          """更新节点的重试计数。当同一个节点被重复执行时递增。"""
          # 只在 current_node 与上次相同时递增
          # 简化处理：每次执行都记录
          prev = state.node_retry_counts.get(node_name, 0)
          state.node_retry_counts[node_name] = prev + 1

      @staticmethod
      def _build_final_output(state: WorkflowState) -> str:
          """组装最终输出。"""
          outputs = state.node_outputs
          last_key = list(outputs.keys())[-1] if outputs else ""
          return outputs.get(last_key, "") if state.status == "completed" else
  "\n".join(state.errors)

  ---
  六、Checkpointer 设计

  # src/haven/workflows/checkpoint.py

  from __future__ import annotations

  import json
  import sqlite3
  import time
  from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
  from dataclasses import asdict
  from pathlib import Path
  from typing import Any


  class Checkpointer(ABC):
      """状态持久化抽象。支持不同的存储后端。"""

      @abstractmethod
      async def save(self, session_id: str, node_name: str, state: Any) -> None:
          ...

      @abstractmethod
      async def load(self, session_id: str) -> Any | None:
          ...

      @abstractmethod
      async def list_sessions(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
          ...


  class SQLiteCheckpointer(Checkpointer):
      """基于 SQLite 的 Checkpointer。

      在现有的 ``.data/memory.db`` 中新增 ``workflow_checkpoints`` 表。
      """

      def __init__(self, db_path: str | Path | None = None):
          if db_path is None:
              from haven.config import settings
              db_path = settings.project_root / ".data" / "memory.db"
          db_path = Path(db_path)
          db_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
          self._conn = sqlite3.connect(str(db_path), check_same_thread=False)
          self._conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
          self._init_schema()

      def _init_schema(self) -> None:
          self._conn.executescript("""
              CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS workflow_checkpoints (
                  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                  session_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                  node_name TEXT NOT NULL,
                  state_json TEXT NOT NULL,
                  created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
              );
              CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_checkpoint_session
                  ON workflow_checkpoints(session_id, created_at DESC);
          """)
          self._conn.commit()

      async def save(self, session_id: str, node_name: str, state: Any) -> None:
          """保存当前状态快照。同 session 同 node 覆盖（只保留最新）。"""
          state_dict = self._serialize(state)
          state_json = json.dumps(state_dict, ensure_ascii=False, default=str)

          # UPSERT: 同 session + node 只保留最新
          self._conn.execute("""
              INSERT INTO workflow_checkpoints (session_id, node_name, 
  state_json)
              VALUES (?, ?, ?)
              ON CONFLICT(session_id, node_name) DO UPDATE SET
                  state_json = excluded.state_json,
                  created_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
          """, (session_id, node_name, state_json))
          self._conn.commit()

      async def load(self, session_id: str) -> Any | None:
          """加载指定 session 的最新 checkpoint。"""
          row = self._conn.execute("""
              SELECT node_name, state_json FROM workflow_checkpoints
              WHERE session_id = ?
              ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1
          """, (session_id,)).fetchone()

          if row is None:
              return None
          return json.loads(row["state_json"])

      async def list_sessions(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
          """列出所有未完成的 session。"""
          rows = self._conn.execute("""
              SELECT session_id, node_name, created_at
              FROM workflow_checkpoints
              WHERE node_name != '__END__'
              ORDER BY created_at DESC
          """).fetchall()
          return [dict(r) for r in rows]

      @staticmethod
      def _serialize(state: Any) -> dict:
          """序列化 state 为 dict，排除不可序列化的字段。"""
          d = asdict(state) if hasattr(state, "__dataclass_fields__") else
  vars(state).copy()
          # 移除不可序列化的字段
          for key in ("_runtime", "messages"):
              if key in d:
                  d[key] = None  # 标记为已清除
          return d


  class MemoryCheckpointer(Checkpointer):
      """内存 checkpointer（用于测试）。"""

      def __init__(self):
          self._store: dict[str, Any] = {}

      async def save(self, session_id: str, node_name: str, state: Any) -> None:
          self._store[session_id] = state

      async def load(self, session_id: str) -> Any | None:
          return self._store.get(session_id)

      async def list_sessions(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
          return [{"session_id": k, "node_name": getattr(v, "current_node", ""),
   "created_at": ""}
                  for k, v in self._store.items()]

  ---
  七、完整示例：软件开发工作流

  7.1 Graph 定义

  # src/haven/workflows/graphs/dev.py

  from haven.workflows.graph import WorkflowGraph
  from haven.workflows.state import DevWorkflowState
  from haven.workflows.nodes import (
      PlannerNode, ArchitectNode, CoderNode, ReviewerNode, TesterNode,
  )
  from haven.workflows.edges import test_router


  def create_dev_workflow() -> WorkflowGraph:
      """创建软件开发工作流。

      Graph:
          planner → architect → coder → reviewer → tester
                                                  │
                                        pass ─────┤
                                                  │ fail (≤3次)
                                                  └──→ coder (重试)
      """

      graph = WorkflowGraph(DevWorkflowState)

      # 注册节点
      graph.add_node("planner", PlannerNode())
      graph.add_node("architect", ArchitectNode())
      graph.add_node("coder", CoderNode())
      graph.add_node("reviewer", ReviewerNode())
      graph.add_node("tester", TesterNode())

      # 无条件边
      graph.add_edge("planner", "architect")
      graph.add_edge("architect", "coder")
      graph.add_edge("coder", "reviewer")
      graph.add_edge("reviewer", "tester")

      # 条件边: 测试失败 → 回到 coder
      graph.add_conditional_edge("tester", test_router)

      # 入口
      graph.set_entry_point("planner")

      # 持久化
      graph.set_checkpointer(SQLiteCheckpointer())

      return graph


  # 注册到 WorkflowRegistry
  from haven.workflows.registry import WorkflowRegistry

  WorkflowRegistry.register("dev_flow", create_dev_workflow)

  7.2 执行流程可视化

  ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │                    Dev Workflow 执行示意                          │
  └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

  Task: "实现一个用户注册 API，包含邮箱验证"

  ┌──────────┐
  │ PLANNER  │  skill: coder
  │          │  → 需求分析文档
  │ (1次)    │     · 功能: 注册 + 验证
  └────┬─────┘     · 技术栈: FastAPI + SQLite + SMTP
       │           · 验收标准: 注册成功返回 JWT
  ┌────▼─────┐
  │ARCHITECT │  skill: coder
  │          │  → 架构设计
  │ (1次)    │     · 分层: router → service → repository
  └────┬─────┘     · API: POST /register, GET /verify/{token}
       │           · 数据模型: User(email, password_hash, verified)
  ┌────▼─────┐
  │  CODER   │  skill: coder
  │          │  → 生成 FastAPI 代码
  │ (1-4次)  │     main.py, models.py, auth.py
  └────┬─────┘
       │
  ┌────▼─────┐
  │ REVIEWER │  skill: code_review
  │          │  → 评分: 55/100 ❌
  │ (1次)    │     阻塞项: 密码未哈希、无输入校验
  └────┬─────┘     → 路由到 coder
       │
  ┌────▼─────┐
  │  CODER   │  retry #1
  │ (第2次)  │  收到 review 反馈 → 修正代码
  └────┬─────┘  添加 bcrypt + pydantic validation
       │
  ┌────▼─────┐
  │ REVIEWER │  → 评分: 78/100 ✅
  │ (1次)    │     无阻塞项
  └────┬─────┘     → 路由到 tester
       │
  ┌────▼─────┐
  │  TESTER  │  skill: coder (code_exec)
  │          │  → 测试: 5/6 通过, 1 失败 ❌
  │ (1次)    │     失败: 邮箱格式校验不通过
  └────┬─────┘     → 路由到 coder
       │
  ┌────▼─────┐
  │  CODER   │  retry #2
  │ (第3次)  │  修正邮箱正则
  └────┬─────┘
       │
  ┌────▼─────┐
  │  TESTER  │  → 测试: 6/6 通过 ✅
  │ (1次)    │
  └────┬─────┘
       │ pass
  ┌────▼─────┐
  │   END    │  最终输出: 完整的注册 API 代码 + 测试报告
  └──────────┘

  总调用: 10 次 LLM (含重试)
  包含: 1 planner + 1 architect + 3 coder + 2 reviewer + 2 tester + 1 结束
  Checkpoint: 每个节点完成后保存，可随时恢复

  ---
  八、完整示例：研究工作流

  8.1 Graph 定义

  # src/haven/workflows/graphs/research.py

  from haven.workflows.graph import WorkflowGraph
  from haven.workflows.state import ResearchWorkflowState, WorkflowState
  from haven.workflows.nodes import WorkflowNode
  from haven.workflows.edges import ConditionalEdge, RouterFunc


  # ==================================================================
  # 专用节点
  # ==================================================================

  class SearchNode(WorkflowNode):
      name = "searcher"
      skill_name = None          # 使用 web_search 工具

      def _build_prompt(self, state: ResearchWorkflowState) -> str:
          return f"""\
  ## 任务：信息搜集

  搜索以下主题的相关信息:

  {state.task}

  要求:
  1. 从多个来源搜集信息
  2. 记录来源 URL
  3. 提炼每个来源的核心观点
  """

      def _process_output(self, output: str, state: ResearchWorkflowState) ->
  dict:
          return {"raw_findings": [output]}


  class AnalyzeNode(WorkflowNode):
      name = "analyst"
      skill_name = "data_analysis"

      def _build_prompt(self, state: ResearchWorkflowState) -> str:
          findings = "\n---\n".join(state.raw_findings)
          return f"""\
  ## 任务：信息分析

  分析以下搜集到的信息，识别关键洞察:

  {findings}

  原始主题: {state.task}

  输出:
  1. 核心发现 (3-5条)
  2. 矛盾观点 (如果有)
  3. 数据可信度评估
  4. 仍存在的知识缺口
  """

      def _process_output(self, output: str, state: ResearchWorkflowState) ->
  dict:
          return {"analyzed_insights": output}


  class SynthesizeNode(WorkflowNode):
      name = "synthesizer"
      skill_name = "summarization"

      def _build_prompt(self, state: ResearchWorkflowState) -> str:
          return f"""\
  ## 任务：撰写研究报告

  基于以下分析撰写结构化调研报告:

  原始主题: {state.task}

  分析结果:
  {state.analyzed_insights}

  原始发现:
  {chr(10).join(state.raw_findings)}

  报告格式:
  # {state.task} — 调研报告

  ## 概述
  ## 核心发现
  ## 详细分析
  ## 矛盾与争议
  ## 结论与建议
  ## 信息来源
  """

      def _process_output(self, output: str, state: ResearchWorkflowState) ->
  dict:
          return {"final_report": output}


  # ==================================================================
  # 路由
  # ==================================================================

  def research_quality_router(state: WorkflowState) -> str:
      """信息充分性路由：检查是否需要更多搜索。"""
      rs = state  # type: ResearchWorkflowState
      search_count = state.node_retry_counts.get("searcher", 0)

      # 分析结果中提到知识缺口 → 再搜索一轮
      if "知识缺口" in rs.analyzed_insights and search_count < 3:
          return "searcher"
      return "synthesizer"


  # ==================================================================
  # 图构建
  # ==================================================================

  def create_research_workflow() -> WorkflowGraph:
      """创建调研工作流。

      Graph:
          searcher → analyst ──┬──→ synthesizer → END
                     ▲         │
                     │  (缺口) │
                     └─────────┘
      """

      graph = WorkflowGraph(ResearchWorkflowState)

      graph.add_node("searcher", SearchNode())
      graph.add_node("analyst", AnalyzeNode())
      graph.add_node("synthesizer", SynthesizeNode())

      graph.add_edge("searcher", "analyst")
      # 条件边: 分析发现有知识缺口 → 回到搜索
      graph.add_conditional_edge("analyst", research_quality_router)

      graph.set_entry_point("searcher")
      graph.set_checkpointer(SQLiteCheckpointer())

      return graph


  WorkflowRegistry.register("research_flow", create_research_workflow)

  8.2 执行流程

  Task: "调研 2026 年 AI Agent 框架的发展趋势"

  ┌──────────┐
  │ SEARCHER │  web_search × N
  │          │  → 搜集到 8 个来源的信息
  │ (1-3次)  │     · LangGraph, CrewAI, AutoGen, Dify...
  └────┬─────┘
       │
  ┌────▼─────┐
  │ ANALYST  │  skill: data_analysis
  │          │  → 分析发现:
  │ (1-3次)  │     · 核心: 多agent编排成主流
  └────┬─────┘     · 缺口: 开源vs商业的成本对比数据不足
       │           → 路由到 searcher
       │
  ┌────▼─────┐
  │ SEARCHER │  retry #1
  │ (第2次)  │  → 专门搜索 "AI agent framework pricing comparison 2026"
  └────┬─────┘
       │
  ┌────▼─────┐
  │ ANALYST  │  → 缺口已填补
  │ (第2次)  │  → 路由到 synthesizer
  └────┬─────┘
       │
  ┌────▼─────┐
  │SYNTHESIZE│  skill: summarization
  │          │  → 生成结构化调研报告
  │ (1次)    │     包含: 概述/发现/分析/结论/来源
  └────┬─────┘
       │
  ┌────▼─────┐
  │   END    │  最终输出: Markdown 格式调研报告
  └──────────┘

  ---
  九、Retry 机制详解

  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │                     Retry 策略                                │
  ├──────────┬──────────────────┬────────────────────────────────┤
  │ 策略     │ 触发条件          │ 行为                           │
  ├──────────┼──────────────────┼────────────────────────────────┤
  │ 直接重试 │ Router 返回       │ 同一节点重新执行                │
  │          │ "__RETRY__"       │ retry_count + 1                │
  ├──────────┼──────────────────┼────────────────────────────────┤
  │ 带反馈   │ Tester fail       │ 回到 Coder，注入 test_failures │
  │ 重试     │ → router → coder │ + review_feedback 到 prompt    │
  ├──────────┼──────────────────┼────────────────────────────────┤
  │ 升级重试 │ retry >= max      │ 跳到 human_review 节点         │
  │          │ (quality_gate)    │ 或直接 fail 结束               │
  ├──────────┼──────────────────┼────────────────────────────────┤
  │ 智能重试 │ LLM 判断          │ Router 用 LLM 分析失败原因      │
  │          │ (analyze_error)   │ 决定: 重试/跳过/降级/失败      │
  └──────────┴──────────────────┴────────────────────────────────┘

  Retry 上限控制:
    - 全局: state.max_retries_per_node (默认 3)
    - 按节点: WorkflowNode.max_retries (覆盖全局)
    - 按 workflow: graph._max_graph_iterations (默认 20, 防死循环)

  ---
  十一、与整体架构的衔接

  User Input
      │
      ▼
  PlannerAgent.execute(task)
      │
      ├─ plan.skills → SkillRegistry.resolve_dependencies()
      ├─ plan.workflow == "dev_flow"
      │       │
      │       ▼
      │  WorkflowGraph.run(state, runtime)
      │       │
      │       ├─ planner_node(state)    → runtime.run(prompt,
  active_skills=["coder"])
      │       ├─ architect_node(state)  → runtime.run(prompt,
  active_skills=["coder"])
      │       ├─ coder_node(state)      → runtime.run(prompt,
  active_skills=["coder"])
      │       ├─ reviewer_node(state)   → runtime.run(prompt,
  active_skills=["code_review"])
      │       ├─ tester_node(state)     → runtime.run(prompt,
  active_skills=["coder"])
      │       │       │
      │       │       ├── pass → END
      │       │       └── fail → coder_node (retry with feedback)
      │       │
      │       └─ return state.final_output
      │
      └─ return result to user

  每个 Node 内部只做一件事：runtime.run(prompt, active_skills, 
  active_tools)。节点的区别仅在于 prompt 内容和使用的 skill。所有执行逻辑复用
  Runtime，零重复代码。
