  ---
  Haven Memory V2 设计方案

  一、架构总览

  ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │                       MemoryManager                              │
  │                                                                  │
  │  retrieve(task, session_id) → MemoryContext                     │
  │  store(turn) → None                                              │
  │  consolidate() → None    (后台)                                  │
  │                                                                  │
  ├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │                                                                  │
  │  ┌──────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐  ┌─────────┐
  │  │   Working    │  │   Episodic   │  │   Semantic   │  │ Vector  │
  │  │   Memory     │  │   Memory     │  │   Memory     │  │ Memory  │
  │  │              │  │              │  │              │  │         │
  │  │  当前上下文   │  │  对话记录     │  │  结构化知识   │  │ 语义检索 │
  │  │  生命周期=    │  │  生命周期=    │  │  生命周期=    │  │ 生命周期=│
  │  │   会话        │  │   永久        │  │   永久        │  │  永久    │
  │  └──────┬───────┘  └──────┬───────┘  └──────┬───────┘  └────┬────┘
  │         │                 │                 │               │
  │         ▼                 ▼                 ▼               ▼
  │  ┌──────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐  ┌─────────┐
  │  │   内存        │  │   SQLite     │  │   SQLite      │  │ChromaDB │
  │  │   deque      │  │ conversations│  │  entities     │  │ /Qdrant │
  │  │              │  │              │  │  entity_facts │  │         │
  │  └──────────────┘  └──────────────┘  └──────────────┘  └─────────┘
  │                                                                  │
  ├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │                                                                  │
  │  Consolidation Pipeline (后台异步)                                │
  │                                                                  │
  │  Episode → summarize → compress → extract facts → store semantic │
  │          → embed chunks → store vector                           │
  │                                                                  │
  └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

  ---
  二、Memory 抽象接口

  2.1 基类

  # src/haven/memory/base.py

  from __future__ import annotations

  from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
  from dataclasses import dataclass, field
  from datetime import datetime
  from typing import Any


  # ============================================================================
  # 统一数据模型
  # ============================================================================

  @dataclass
  class MemoryItem:
      """所有 Memory 类型的统一条目。"""

      id: str                                    # 唯一标识
      content: str                               # 主要内容
      memory_type: str                           # working | episodic | semantic
   | vector
      created_at: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
      importance: float = 0.5                    # 重要性评分 (0-1)，影响 
  retention
      access_count: int = 0                      # 被检索次数
      last_accessed_at: datetime | None = None   # 最后被检索时间
      metadata: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
      embedding: list[float] | None = None       # 向量（Vector Memory 使用）


  @dataclass
  class MemoryContext:
      """MemoryManager.retrieve() 的返回结果。

      包含各 Memory 类型的检索结果，供 PromptBuilder 组装。
      """

      working: list[MemoryItem] = field(default_factory=list)    # 最近 N 轮消息
      episodic: list[MemoryItem] = field(default_factory=list)   # 
  相关历史对话片段
      semantic: list[MemoryItem] = field(default_factory=list)   # 结构化事实
      vector: list[MemoryItem] = field(default_factory=list)     # 语义相似内容

      def format_for_prompt(self) -> str:
          """格式化为 system prompt 注入片段。"""
          parts: list[str] = []

          if self.semantic:
              lines = ["\n[长期记忆 — 关于当前用户]"]
              for item in self.semantic:
                  lines.append(f"- {item.content}")
              parts.append("\n".join(lines))

          if self.episodic:
              lines = ["\n[相关历史对话]"]
              for item in self.episodic:
                  ts = item.created_at.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M")
                  lines.append(f"[{ts}] {item.content}")
              parts.append("\n".join(lines))

          if self.vector:
              lines = ["\n[相关知识库片段]"]
              for item in self.vector:
                  lines.append(item.content)
              parts.append("\n".join(lines))

          return "\n".join(parts)

      @property
      def total_tokens_estimate(self) -> int:
          """估算注入 prompt 的总 token 数（按 1 token ≈ 3 chars 估算）。"""
          text = self.format_for_prompt()
          return len(text) // 3


  # ============================================================================
  # 抽象接口
  # ============================================================================

  class BaseMemory(ABC):
      """Memory 存储的抽象基类。

      每种 Memory 类型（Working/Episodic/Semantic/Vector）实现此接口。
      """

      name: str = "base"

      @abstractmethod
      async def store(self, items: list[MemoryItem]) -> None:
          """存储一批条目。"""
          ...

      @abstractmethod
      async def retrieve(self, query: str, top_k: int = 5, **filters: Any) ->
  list[MemoryItem]:
          """按 query 检索 top_k 条目。"""
          ...

      @abstractmethod
      async def forget(self, item_id: str) -> None:
          """删除指定条目。"""
          ...

      @abstractmethod
      async def clear(self) -> None:
          """清空此 Memory 的全部数据。"""
          ...

      async def consolidate(self) -> int:
          """后台整合。返回整合条目数。默认不做任何事。"""
          return 0

  ---
  三、四种 Memory 实现

  3.1 Working Memory

  Working Memory = 当前会话的活跃上下文窗口

  特性:
    · 生命周期 = 会话（进程内存，不持久化）
    · 容量 = 最近 N 轮完整消息 + 当前轮的工具调用消息
    · 结构 = sliding window (最新优先) + priority pinned items
    · 注入 = 直接作为 messages 列表传给 LLM

  # src/haven/memory/working.py

  from __future__ import annotations

  from collections import deque
  from datetime import datetime
  from typing import Any

  from langchain_core.messages import BaseMessage

  from haven.memory.base import BaseMemory, MemoryItem


  class WorkingMemory(BaseMemory):
      """当前会话上下文。不持久化。

      结构:
        - sliding_window: deque[BaseMessage] — 最近 N 条消息
        - pinned_items:   list[MemoryItem]    — 重要信息（始终保留）
        - summary:        str                 — 超出窗口的消息摘要

      注入方式:
        - sliding_window 中的消息直接追加到 LLM messages 列表
        - pinned_items 注入到 system prompt
        - summary 注入到 system prompt
      """

      name = "working"

      def __init__(self, max_messages: int = 100, summary_threshold: int = 80):
          self.max_messages = max_messages
          self.summary_threshold = summary_threshold
          self.sliding_window: deque[BaseMessage] = deque(maxlen=max_messages)
          self.pinned_items: list[MemoryItem] = []
          self.summary: str = ""          # 超出窗口的消息的滚动摘要
          self._summary_buffer: list[BaseMessage] = []  # 待摘要的消息

      # ========== BaseMemory 接口 ==========

      async def store(self, items: list[MemoryItem]) -> None:
          """不直接使用。消息通过 add_message() 添加。"""
          pass

      async def retrieve(self, query: str = "", top_k: int = 5, **filters: Any)
  -> list[MemoryItem]:
          """返回当前窗口的消息 + pinned items。"""
          items: list[MemoryItem] = list(self.pinned_items)

          # 将最近的 top_k 条消息转为 MemoryItem
          for msg in list(self.sliding_window)[-top_k:]:
              items.append(MemoryItem(
                  id=f"working_{id(msg)}",
                  content=msg.content if hasattr(msg, "content") else str(msg),
                  memory_type="working",
                  importance=0.8,
              ))

          return items

      async def forget(self, item_id: str) -> None:
          self.pinned_items = [i for i in self.pinned_items if i.id != item_id]

      async def clear(self) -> None:
          self.sliding_window.clear()
          self.pinned_items.clear()
          self.summary = ""
          self._summary_buffer.clear()

      # ========== 专用 API ==========

      def add_message(self, message: BaseMessage) -> None:
          """添加消息到滑动窗口。触发摘要检查。"""
          self.sliding_window.append(message)

          # 旧消息进入摘要缓冲区
          if len(self.sliding_window) >= self.max_messages:
              overflow = self.sliding_window.popleft()
              self._summary_buffer.append(overflow)

      def add_messages(self, messages: list[BaseMessage]) -> None:
          for msg in messages:
              self.add_message(msg)

      def pin(self, item: MemoryItem) -> None:
          """固定重要信息，始终保留在 prompt 中。"""
          self.pinned_items.append(item)

      def unpin(self, item_id: str) -> None:
          self.pinned_items = [i for i in self.pinned_items if i.id != item_id]

      def get_messages(self) -> list[BaseMessage]:
          """返回当前窗口的全部消息。"""
          return list(self.sliding_window)

      def needs_summarization(self) -> bool:
          """是否需要触发摘要压缩。"""
          return len(self._summary_buffer) >= 10

      async def summarize(self, llm: Any) -> str:
          """压缩超出窗口的消息为滚动摘要。不阻塞主流程。"""
          if not self._summary_buffer:
              return self.summary

          buffer_content = "\n".join(
              m.content if hasattr(m, "content") else str(m)
              for m in self._summary_buffer
          )

          prompt = f"""\
  总结以下历史对话的要点，保留关键信息。用中文，不超过 200 字:

  {self.summary + chr(10) if self.summary else ""}
  {buffer_content}

  要点:"""

          try:
              from langchain_core.messages import HumanMessage
              response = await llm.ainvoke([HumanMessage(content=prompt)])
              new_summary = response.content if hasattr(response, "content")
  else str(response)
          except Exception:
              new_summary = self.summary

          self.summary = new_summary
          self._summary_buffer.clear()
          return new_summary

  3.2 Episodic Memory

  Episodic Memory = "发生了什么，说过了什么"

  存储单位: Episode = 一轮对话 (user_message + assistant_response + 元数据)

  特性:
    · 每轮对话完整存储
    · 带时间戳 + session_id + 重要性评分
    · 支持关键词检索 + 时间范围检索
    · 旧 episode 自动生成摘要（consolidation）

  # src/haven/memory/episodic.py

  from __future__ import annotations

  import json
  import sqlite3
  from datetime import datetime, timedelta
  from pathlib import Path
  from typing import Any

  from haven.memory.base import BaseMemory, MemoryItem


  class EpisodicMemory(BaseMemory):
      """对话记录存储。完整保留每轮对话。

      存储后端: SQLite (episodes 表)

      检索策略:
        1. 关键词匹配 (content LIKE %query%)
        2. 时间衰减 (最近优先)
        3. 重要性加权 (importance 高的优先)
      """

      name = "episodic"

      def __init__(self, db_path: str | Path | None = None):
          if db_path is None:
              from haven.config import settings
              db_path = settings.project_root / ".data" / "memory.db"
          db_path = Path(db_path)
          db_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
          self._conn = sqlite3.connect(str(db_path), check_same_thread=False)
          self._conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
          self._init_schema()

      def _init_schema(self) -> None:
          self._conn.executescript("""
              CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS episodes (
                  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                  session_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                  turn_number INTEGER NOT NULL,
                  user_message TEXT NOT NULL,
                  assistant_response TEXT NOT NULL,
                  summary TEXT DEFAULT '',
                  importance REAL DEFAULT 0.5,
                  metadata_json TEXT DEFAULT '{}',
                  created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
              );

              CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_episodes_session
                  ON episodes(session_id, turn_number);
              CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_episodes_importance
                  ON episodes(importance DESC);
              CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_episodes_created
                  ON episodes(created_at DESC);
          """)
          self._conn.commit()

      # ========== 存储 ==========

      async def store(self, items: list[MemoryItem]) -> None:
          for item in items:
              meta_json = json.dumps(item.metadata, ensure_ascii=False)
              self._conn.execute("""
                  INSERT OR REPLACE INTO episodes
                      (id, session_id, turn_number, user_message, 
  assistant_response,
                       summary, importance, metadata_json)
                  VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
              """, (
                  item.id,
                  item.metadata.get("session_id", "default"),
                  item.metadata.get("turn_number", 0),
                  item.metadata.get("user_message", ""),
                  item.content,
                  item.metadata.get("summary", ""),
                  item.importance,
                  meta_json,
              ))
          self._conn.commit()

      # 便捷方法
      async def store_turn(
          self,
          session_id: str,
          turn_number: int,
          user_message: str,
          assistant_response: str,
          importance: float = 0.5,
      ) -> None:
          from uuid import uuid4
          item = MemoryItem(
              id=f"ep_{session_id}_{turn_number}_{uuid4().hex[:8]}",
              content=assistant_response,
              memory_type="episodic",
              importance=importance,
              metadata={
                  "session_id": session_id,
                  "turn_number": turn_number,
                  "user_message": user_message,
              },
          )
          await self.store([item])

      # ========== 检索 ==========

      async def retrieve(self, query: str = "", top_k: int = 5, **filters: Any)
  -> list[MemoryItem]:
          """多策略混合检索。

          filters:
            - session_id: str       — 限定会话
            - time_range: str       — "7d" / "24h" / "30d"
            - min_importance: float — 最低重要性
            - search_mode: str      — "keyword" / "recent" / "hybrid"
          """
          session_id = filters.get("session_id")
          time_range = filters.get("time_range")
          min_importance = filters.get("min_importance", 0.0)
          search_mode = filters.get("search_mode", "hybrid")

          where_clauses: list[str] = ["1=1"]
          params: list[Any] = []

          if session_id:
              where_clauses.append("session_id = ?")
              params.append(session_id)

          if time_range:
              delta = self._parse_time_range(time_range)
              cutoff = datetime.now() - delta
              where_clauses.append("created_at >= ?")
              params.append(cutoff.isoformat())

          if min_importance > 0:
              where_clauses.append("importance >= ?")
              params.append(min_importance)

          where = " AND ".join(where_clauses)

          if search_mode == "recent":
              # 纯时间排序
              order = "created_at DESC"
              extra_params: list[Any] = []
          elif search_mode == "keyword" and query:
              # 关键词匹配 + 重要性
              where += " AND (user_message LIKE ? OR assistant_response LIKE ? 
  OR summary LIKE ?)"
              kw = f"%{query}%"
              extra_params = [kw, kw, kw]
              order = "importance DESC, created_at DESC"
          else:
              # hybrid: 时间衰减 × 重要性
              extra_params = []
              order = """
                  (importance * 0.6 + (1.0 / (julianday('now') - 
  julianday(created_at) + 1.0)) * 0.4) DESC
              """

          rows = self._conn.execute(
              f"SELECT * FROM episodes WHERE {where} ORDER BY {order} LIMIT ?",
              [*params, *extra_params, top_k],
          ).fetchall()

          return [self._row_to_item(r, query) for r in rows]

      # ========== 遗忘 ==========

      async def forget(self, item_id: str) -> None:
          self._conn.execute("DELETE FROM episodes WHERE id = ?", (item_id,))
          self._conn.commit()

      async def clear(self) -> None:
          self._conn.execute("DELETE FROM episodes")
          self._conn.commit()

      # ========== Consolidation ==========

      async def consolidate(self, llm: Any = None) -> int:
          """为未压缩的旧 episode 生成摘要。"""
          rows = self._conn.execute("""
              SELECT id, user_message, assistant_response
              FROM episodes
              WHERE summary = '' AND created_at < datetime('now', '-1 day')
              LIMIT 20
          """).fetchall()

          if not rows or llm is None:
              return 0

          count = 0
          for row in rows:
              summary = await self._generate_summary(
                  llm, row["user_message"], row["assistant_response"]
              )
              if summary:
                  self._conn.execute(
                      "UPDATE episodes SET summary = ? WHERE id = ?",
                      (summary, row["id"]),
                  )
                  count += 1

          self._conn.commit()
          return count

      # ========== 内部 ==========

      async def _generate_summary(self, llm: Any, user: str, assistant: str) ->
  str:
          try:
              from langchain_core.messages import HumanMessage
              prompt = f"用一句话总结这段对话的关键信息:\n用户: {user}\nAI:
  {assistant[:500]}\n\n总结:"
              response = await llm.ainvoke([HumanMessage(content=prompt)])
              return (response.content if hasattr(response, "content") else
  str(response)).strip()
          except Exception:
              return f"用户询问: {user[:100]}"

      @staticmethod
      def _parse_time_range(s: str) -> timedelta:
          s = s.strip().lower()
          if s.endswith("d"):
              return timedelta(days=int(s[:-1]))
          elif s.endswith("h"):
              return timedelta(hours=int(s[:-1]))
          return timedelta(days=7)

      def _row_to_item(self, row: Any, query: str) -> MemoryItem:
          metadata = json.loads(row["metadata_json"]) if row["metadata_json"]
  else {}
          metadata["session_id"] = row["session_id"]
          metadata["turn_number"] = row["turn_number"]

          # 构造可读的 content
          parts = [
              f"用户: {row['user_message'][:200]}",
              f"AI: {row['summary'] or row['assistant_response'][:200]}",
          ]
          content = "\n".join(parts)

          return MemoryItem(
              id=row["id"],
              content=content,
              memory_type="episodic",
              created_at=datetime.fromisoformat(row["created_at"]),
              importance=row["importance"],
              metadata=metadata,
          )

  3.3 Semantic Memory

  Semantic Memory = "我知道什么"

  存储单位: Fact = (entity, key, value, confidence, source)

  升级点 (vs 当前 entity_facts):
    · 多实体：user, project, domain_knowledge
    · 关系：fact 之间可以关联 (fact_id → related_fact_ids)
    · 来源追溯：每条 fact 记录来源 session + turn
    · 时间线：同 key 历史版本可查
    · 置信度衰减：旧事实如未被再提及，置信度下降

  # src/haven/memory/semantic.py

  from __future__ import annotations

  import json
  import sqlite3
  from datetime import datetime
  from pathlib import Path
  from typing import Any

  from haven.memory.base import BaseMemory, MemoryItem


  class SemanticMemory(BaseMemory):
      """结构化知识存储。实体-关系-事实图。

      存储后端: SQLite (新 schema)

      Schema:
        entities    — 实体对象 (user, project, topic, agent...)
        facts       — 事实 (entity_id, key, value, confidence, source, 
  related_facts)
        fact_history — 事实变更历史 (同 key 被覆盖时的旧值存档)
      """

      name = "semantic"

      def __init__(self, db_path: str | Path | None = None):
          if db_path is None:
              from haven.config import settings
              db_path = settings.project_root / ".data" / "memory.db"
          db_path = Path(db_path)
          db_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
          self._conn = sqlite3.connect(str(db_path), check_same_thread=False)
          self._conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
          self._init_schema()

      def _init_schema(self) -> None:
          self._conn.executescript("""
              -- 实体
              CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS memory_entities (
                  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                  name TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
                  type TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'user',
                  description TEXT DEFAULT '',
                  created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                  updated_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
              );

              -- 事实（增强版）
              CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS memory_facts (
                  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                  entity_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES memory_entities(id),
                  key TEXT NOT NULL,
                  value TEXT NOT NULL,
                  confidence REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.9,
                  source_session TEXT DEFAULT '',
                  source_turn INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
                  related_fact_ids TEXT DEFAULT '[]',
                  tags TEXT DEFAULT '[]',
                  created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                  UNIQUE(entity_id, key)
              );

              -- 事实变更历史
              CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS memory_fact_history (
                  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                  fact_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES memory_facts(id),
                  old_value TEXT NOT NULL,
                  new_value TEXT NOT NULL,
                  changed_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
              );

              CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_memory_facts_entity
                  ON memory_facts(entity_id);
              CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_memory_facts_key
                  ON memory_facts(key);
              CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_memory_facts_confidence
                  ON memory_facts(confidence);
              CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_memory_entities_type
                  ON memory_entities(type);
          """)
          self._conn.commit()

      # ========== 存储 ==========

      async def store(self, items: list[MemoryItem]) -> None:
          """存储结构化事实。metadata 中需包含 {entity_name, key, value, 
  confidence}。"""
          for item in items:
              entity_name = item.metadata.get("entity_name", "user")
              key = item.metadata.get("key", "")
              value = item.metadata.get("value", "") or item.content
              entity_type = item.metadata.get("entity_type", "user")

              if not key or not value:
                  continue

              entity_id = self._get_or_create_entity(entity_name, entity_type)
              old_value = self._get_fact_value(entity_id, key)

              # 写入或更新事实
              if old_value is not None and old_value != value:
                  # 记录历史
                  self._record_history(entity_id, key, old_value, value)

              self._upsert_fact(
                  entity_id, key, value,
                  confidence=item.metadata.get("confidence", item.importance),
                  source_session=item.metadata.get("source_session", ""),
                  source_turn=item.metadata.get("source_turn", 0),

  related_fact_ids=json.dumps(item.metadata.get("related_fact_ids", [])),
                  tags=json.dumps(item.metadata.get("tags", [])),
              )

      # ========== 检索 ==========

      async def retrieve(self, query: str = "", top_k: int = 10, **filters: Any)
   -> list[MemoryItem]:
          """检索结构化事实。

          filters:
            - entity_name: str     — 按实体过滤
            - key: str             — 按 key 过滤
            - min_confidence: float
            - tag: str             — 按标签过滤
          """
          entity_name = filters.get("entity_name")
          key = filters.get("key")
          min_confidence = filters.get("min_confidence", 0.3)
          tag = filters.get("tag")

          where = ["f.confidence >= ?"]
          params: list[Any] = [min_confidence]

          joins = ["JOIN memory_entities e ON f.entity_id = e.id"]

          if entity_name:
              where.append("e.name = ?")
              params.append(entity_name)
          if key:
              where.append("f.key = ?")
              params.append(key)
          if tag:
              where.append("f.tags LIKE ?")
              params.append(f'%"{tag}"%')
          if query:
              where.append("(f.key LIKE ? OR f.value LIKE ?)")
              kw = f"%{query}%"
              params.extend([kw, kw])

          where_clause = " AND ".join(where)
          join_clause = " ".join(joins)

          rows = self._conn.execute(f"""
              SELECT f.*, e.name as entity_name, e.type as entity_type
              FROM memory_facts f
              {join_clause}
              WHERE {where_clause}
              ORDER BY f.confidence DESC, f.created_at DESC
              LIMIT ?
          """, [*params, top_k]).fetchall()

          return [self._row_to_item(r) for r in rows]

      async def retrieve_entity_facts(self, entity_name: str) ->
  list[MemoryItem]:
          """获取一个实体的全部已知事实。"""
          return await self.retrieve(entity_name=entity_name, top_k=50)

      # ========== 遗忘 ==========

      async def forget(self, item_id: str) -> None:
          self._conn.execute("DELETE FROM memory_facts WHERE id = ?",
  (int(item_id),))
          self._conn.commit()

      async def clear(self) -> None:
          self._conn.execute("DELETE FROM memory_facts")
          self._conn.execute("DELETE FROM memory_entities")
          self._conn.execute("DELETE FROM memory_fact_history")
          self._conn.commit()

      # ========== Consolidation ==========

      async def consolidate(self, llm: Any = None) -> int:
          """降低低置信度事实的置信度（衰减），删除过时事实。"""
          # 衰减: 超过 30 天未更新的低置信度事实
          self._conn.execute("""
              UPDATE memory_facts
              SET confidence = MAX(0.1, confidence - 0.1)
              WHERE confidence < 0.9
                AND created_at < datetime('now', '-30 days')
          """)
          # 删除置信度归 0 的事实
          self._conn.execute("DELETE FROM memory_facts WHERE confidence <= 0.1")
          self._conn.commit()
          return self._conn.total_changes

      # ========== 内部 ==========

      def _get_or_create_entity(self, name: str, entity_type: str) -> int:
          self._conn.execute(
              "INSERT OR IGNORE INTO memory_entities (name, type) VALUES (?, 
  ?)",
              (name, entity_type),
          )
          self._conn.commit()
          row = self._conn.execute(
              "SELECT id FROM memory_entities WHERE name = ?", (name,)
          ).fetchone()
          return row["id"]

      def _get_fact_value(self, entity_id: int, key: str) -> str | None:
          row = self._conn.execute(
              "SELECT value FROM memory_facts WHERE entity_id = ? AND key = ?",
              (entity_id, key),
          ).fetchone()
          return row["value"] if row else None

      def _record_history(self, entity_id: int, key: str, old_value: str, 
  new_value: str) -> None:
          fact_row = self._conn.execute(
              "SELECT id FROM memory_facts WHERE entity_id = ? AND key = ?",
              (entity_id, key),
          ).fetchone()
          if fact_row:
              self._conn.execute(
                  "INSERT INTO memory_fact_history (fact_id, old_value, 
  new_value) VALUES (?, ?, ?)",
                  (fact_row["id"], old_value, new_value),
              )
              self._conn.commit()

      def _upsert_fact(self, entity_id: int, key: str, value: str, **kwargs: 
  Any) -> None:
          self._conn.execute("""
              INSERT INTO memory_facts
                  (entity_id, key, value, confidence, source_session, 
  source_turn,
                   related_fact_ids, tags)
              VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
              ON CONFLICT(entity_id, key) DO UPDATE SET
                  value = excluded.value,
                  confidence = excluded.confidence,
                  source_session = excluded.source_session,
                  source_turn = excluded.source_turn,
                  related_fact_ids = excluded.related_fact_ids,
                  tags = excluded.tags,
                  created_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
          """, (
              entity_id, key, value,
              kwargs.get("confidence", 0.9),
              kwargs.get("source_session", ""),
              kwargs.get("source_turn", 0),
              kwargs.get("related_fact_ids", "[]"),
              kwargs.get("tags", "[]"),
          ))
          self._conn.commit()
          # 更新实体的 updated_at
          self._conn.execute(
              "UPDATE memory_entities SET updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP WHERE 
  id = ?",
              (entity_id,),
          )
          self._conn.commit()

      def _row_to_item(self, row: Any) -> MemoryItem:
          return MemoryItem(
              id=str(row["id"]),
              content=f"{row['key']}: {row['value']}",
              memory_type="semantic",
              created_at=datetime.fromisoformat(row["created_at"]),
              importance=row["confidence"],
              access_count=0,
              metadata={
                  "entity_name": row["entity_name"],
                  "entity_type": row["entity_type"],
                  "key": row["key"],
                  "value": row["value"],
                  "confidence": row["confidence"],
                  "source_session": row["source_session"],
                  "source_turn": row["source_turn"],
              },
          )

  3.4 Vector Memory

  Vector Memory = 语义相似性检索

  存储单位: Chunk = (text, embedding)

  用途:
    · 对话片段语义检索 ("用户说过关于 Python 的事情")
    · 知识库语义检索 (RAG)
    · 跨 session 模式匹配 ("类似的问题之前是怎么解决的")

  检索策略:
    · embedding 相似度 (cosine)
    · metadata 过滤 (session, date, topic)

  # src/haven/memory/vector.py

  from __future__ import annotations

  import logging
  from datetime import datetime
  from typing import Any

  from haven.memory.base import BaseMemory, MemoryItem

  logger = logging.getLogger("haven.memory.vector")


  class VectorMemory(BaseMemory):
      """向量语义记忆。基于 embedding 的相似性检索。

      存储后端: ChromaDB（持久化本地文件）

      默认使用 OpenAI 兼容的 embedding API。
      """

      name = "vector"

      def __init__(
          self,
          collection_name: str = "haven_memory",
          persist_dir: str | None = None,
          embedding_model: str = "text-embedding-3-small",
          embedding_api_base: str = "https://api.openai.com/v1",
          embedding_api_key: str = "",
      ):
          self._collection_name = collection_name
          self._embedding_model = embedding_model
          self._embedding_api_base = embedding_api_base
          self._embedding_api_key = embedding_api_key
          self._client = None
          self._collection = None

          if persist_dir is None:
              from haven.config import settings
              persist_dir = str(settings.project_root / ".data" / "chroma")

          self._persist_dir = persist_dir
          self._embedding_fn = None

      # ========== 延迟初始化 ==========

      def _ensure_client(self):
          if self._client is not None:
              return
          try:
              import chromadb
              self._client = chromadb.PersistentClient(path=self._persist_dir)
              self._collection = self._client.get_or_create_collection(
                  name=self._collection_name,
                  metadata={"hnsw:space": "cosine"},
              )
          except ImportError:
              logger.warning("chromadb not installed, VectorMemory disabled")
              self._client = False  # 标记为不可用
              self._collection = None

      def _ensure_embedding_fn(self):
          if self._embedding_fn is not None:
              return
          try:
              from langchain_openai import OpenAIEmbeddings
              self._embedding_fn = OpenAIEmbeddings(
                  model=self._embedding_model,
                  openai_api_base=self._embedding_api_base,
                  openai_api_key=self._embedding_api_key or "none",
              )
          except Exception:
              logger.warning("Embedding function init failed, VectorMemory 
  disabled")
              self._embedding_fn = False

      @property
      def _available(self) -> bool:
          return self._client is not False and self._embedding_fn is not False

      # ========== 存储 ==========

      async def store(self, items: list[MemoryItem]) -> None:
          if not items:
              return
          self._ensure_client()
          self._ensure_embedding_fn()
          if not self._available:
              return

          texts = [item.content for item in items]
          ids = [item.id for item in items]
          metadatas = [
              {
                  "memory_type": item.memory_type,
                  "importance": item.importance,
                  "created_at": item.created_at.isoformat(),
                  **{f"meta_{k}": str(v)[:1000] for k, v in
  item.metadata.items()},
              }
              for item in items
          ]

          try:
              embeddings = await self._embedding_fn.aembed_documents(texts)
              self._collection.add(
                  ids=ids,
                  embeddings=embeddings,
                  documents=texts,
                  metadatas=metadatas,
              )
          except Exception as exc:
              logger.warning("VectorMemory store failed: %s", exc)

      # ========== 检索 ==========

      async def retrieve(self, query: str = "", top_k: int = 5, **filters: Any)
  -> list[MemoryItem]:
          if not query:
              return []

          self._ensure_client()
          self._ensure_embedding_fn()
          if not self._available:
              return []

          # 构建 where filter
          where_filter = self._build_where_filter(filters)

          try:
              query_embedding = await self._embedding_fn.aembed_query(query)
              results = self._collection.query(
                  query_embeddings=[query_embedding],
                  n_results=top_k,
                  where=where_filter if where_filter else None,
              )
          except Exception as exc:
              logger.warning("VectorMemory retrieve failed: %s", exc)
              return []

          items: list[MemoryItem] = []
          if results and results["ids"] and results["ids"][0]:
              for i, doc_id in enumerate(results["ids"][0]):
                  doc = results["documents"][0][i] if results["documents"] else
  ""
                  meta = results["metadatas"][0][i] if results["metadatas"] else
   {}
                  distance = results["distances"][0][i] if results["distances"]
  else 0.0
                  # cosine distance → similarity score
                  score = 1.0 - distance

                  items.append(MemoryItem(
                      id=doc_id,
                      content=doc,
                      memory_type="vector",
                      importance=meta.get("importance", 0.5),
                      metadata=meta,
                      created_at=datetime.fromisoformat(meta["created_at"])
                          if "created_at" in meta else datetime.now(),
                  ))
                  items[-1].access_count = 1
                  # 将 score 存到 metadata
                  items[-1].metadata["_score"] = score

          return items

      # ========== 遗忘 ==========

      async def forget(self, item_id: str) -> None:
          self._ensure_client()
          if self._available and self._collection:
              try:
                  self._collection.delete(ids=[item_id])
              except Exception:
                  pass

      async def clear(self) -> None:
          self._ensure_client()
          if self._available and self._client:
              try:
                  self._client.delete_collection(self._collection_name)
                  self._collection = self._client.get_or_create_collection(
                      name=self._collection_name,
                      metadata={"hnsw:space": "cosine"},
                  )
              except Exception:
                  pass

      # ========== Consolidation ==========

      async def consolidate(self, llm: Any = None) -> int:
          """清理低相关性的旧条目（保留最近 10000 条）。"""
          self._ensure_client()
          if not self._available or not self._collection:
              return 0
          try:
              count = self._collection.count()
              if count > 10000:
                  # 获取最旧的条目并删除
                  oldest = self._collection.get(limit=count - 10000,
                                                 include=[])
                  if oldest and oldest["ids"]:
                      self._collection.delete(ids=oldest["ids"])
                      return len(oldest["ids"])
          except Exception:
              pass
          return 0

      # ========== 内部 ==========

      @staticmethod
      def _build_where_filter(filters: dict) -> dict | None:
          """构建 ChromaDB where filter。"""
          conditions: list[dict] = []

          if "session_id" in filters:
              conditions.append({"meta_session_id": filters["session_id"]})
          if "memory_type" in filters:
              conditions.append({"memory_type": filters["memory_type"]})
          if "min_importance" in filters:
              conditions.append({"importance": {"$gte":
  filters["min_importance"]}})

          if not conditions:
              return None
          if len(conditions) == 1:
              return conditions[0]
          return {"$and": conditions}

  ---
  四、MemoryManager

  4.1 设计

  # src/haven/memory/manager.py

  from __future__ import annotations

  import asyncio
  import logging
  import uuid
  from datetime import datetime
  from typing import Any

  from langchain_core.language_models import BaseChatModel
  from langchain_core.messages import BaseMessage

  from haven.memory.base import BaseMemory, MemoryContext, MemoryItem
  from haven.memory.working import WorkingMemory
  from haven.memory.episodic import EpisodicMemory
  from haven.memory.semantic import SemanticMemory
  from haven.memory.vector import VectorMemory

  logger = logging.getLogger("haven.memory")


  class MemoryManager:
      """统一记忆管理。编排四种 Memory，管理存储、检索、整合生命周期。

      用法::

          mm = MemoryManager(session_id="abc", entity_name="user_1")
          
          # 每轮对话后
          await mm.record_turn(user_msg="你好", assistant_msg="你好！")
          
          # 每次 LLM 调用前
          ctx = await mm.retrieve(task="帮我写代码", top_k=5)
          system_prompt += ctx.format_for_prompt()
          
          # 后台（定时或每 N 轮）
          await mm.consolidate(llm)
      """

      def __init__(
          self,
          session_id: str = "default",
          entity_name: str = "user",
          channel: str = "cli",
          *,
          enable_vector: bool = True,
          max_working_messages: int = 100,
          summary_threshold: int = 80,
      ):
          self.session_id = session_id
          self.entity_name = entity_name
          self.channel = channel
          self.turn_count: int = 0

          # 四种 Memory 实例
          self.working = WorkingMemory(
              max_messages=max_working_messages,
              summary_threshold=summary_threshold,
          )
          self.episodic = EpisodicMemory()
          self.semantic = SemanticMemory()
          self.vector = VectorMemory() if enable_vector else None

          # LLM 引用（用于摘要生成和事实提取）
          self._llm: BaseChatModel | None = None

      def set_llm(self, llm: BaseChatModel) -> None:
          self._llm = llm

      # ==================================================================
      # 存储 — 每轮对话后调用
      # ==================================================================

      async def record_turn(
          self,
          user_message: str,
          assistant_message: str,
          *,
          importance: float | None = None,
          persist: bool = True,
      ) -> None:
          """记录一轮完整对话到所有 Memory 层。

          Working  — 立即追加消息
          Episodic — 持久化完整对话
          Semantic — 后台提取事实（异步）
          Vector   — embed 本轮对话
          """
          from langchain_core.messages import HumanMessage, AIMessage

          self.turn_count += 1

          # 计算重要性
          if importance is None:
              importance = self._estimate_importance(user_message,
  assistant_message)

          # 1. Working Memory — 追加消息
          self.working.add_message(HumanMessage(content=user_message))
          self.working.add_message(AIMessage(content=assistant_message))

          if not persist:
              return

          # 2. Episodic Memory — 持久化
          await self.episodic.store_turn(
              session_id=self.session_id,
              turn_number=self.turn_count,
              user_message=user_message,
              assistant_response=assistant_message,
              importance=importance,
          )

          # 3. Vector Memory — embed 对话片段
          if self.vector:
              await self.vector.store([
                  MemoryItem(

  id=f"vec_{self.session_id}_{self.turn_count}_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}",
                      content=f"用户: {user_message}\nAI: {assistant_message}",
                      memory_type="episodic",
                      importance=importance,
                      metadata={
                          "session_id": self.session_id,
                          "turn_number": self.turn_count,
                          "entity_name": self.entity_name,
                      },
                  )
              ])

          # 4. Semantic Memory — 异步提取事实（不阻塞）
          asyncio.create_task(self._extract_facts(user_message,
  assistant_message))

          # 5. Working Memory — 检查是否需要摘要压缩
          if self.working.needs_summarization() and self._llm:
              asyncio.create_task(self.working.summarize(self._llm))

      # ==================================================================
      # 检索 — 每次 LLM 调用前
      # ==================================================================

      async def retrieve(
          self,
          task: str = "",
          *,
          top_k: int = 5,
          search_episodic: bool = True,
          search_semantic: bool = True,
          search_vector: bool = True,
          time_range: str = "30d",
          min_importance: float = 0.3,
      ) -> MemoryContext:
          """多路并行检索，合并去重。

          检索策略:
            - Semantic 始终检索（轻量 SQL，获取用户事实）
            - Episodic 检索相关历史对话
            - Vector 语义搜索相似内容
            - Working 始终包含（当前上下文）
          """
          tasks: list[asyncio.Task] = []

          # 始终包含 Working
          tasks.append(asyncio.create_task(
              self.working.retrieve(task="", top_k=20)
          ))

          # Semantic（轻量，始终执行）
          if search_semantic:
              tasks.append(asyncio.create_task(
                  self.semantic.retrieve(
                      query=task if task else self.entity_name,
                      top_k=10,
                      entity_name=self.entity_name,
                      min_confidence=min_importance,
                  )
              ))
          else:
              tasks.append(asyncio.create_task(
                  self._empty_result()
              ))

          # Episodic（中等开销）
          if search_episodic:
              tasks.append(asyncio.create_task(
                  self.episodic.retrieve(
                      query=task,
                      top_k=top_k,
                      session_id=None,  # 跨会话检索
                      time_range=time_range,
                      min_importance=min_importance,
                      search_mode="hybrid",
                  )
              ))
          else:
              tasks.append(asyncio.create_task(self._empty_result()))

          # Vector（稍高开销，embedding API 调用）
          if search_vector and self.vector and task:
              tasks.append(asyncio.create_task(
                  self.vector.retrieve(
                      query=task,
                      top_k=top_k,
                      min_importance=min_importance,
                  )
              ))
          else:
              tasks.append(asyncio.create_task(self._empty_result()))

          results = await asyncio.gather(*tasks, return_exceptions=True)

          working_items = results[0] if not isinstance(results[0], Exception)
  else []
          semantic_items = results[1] if not isinstance(results[1], Exception)
  else []
          episodic_items = results[2] if not isinstance(results[2], Exception)
  else []
          vector_items = results[3] if not isinstance(results[3], Exception)
  else []

          return MemoryContext(
              working=working_items,
              semantic=semantic_items,
              episodic=episodic_items,
              vector=vector_items,
          )

      # ==================================================================
      # Consolidation — 后台整合
      # ==================================================================

      async def consolidate(self, llm: BaseChatModel | None = None) -> dict[str,
   int]:
          """后台整合所有 Memory 层。返回各层整合条目数。

          建议：每 10 轮对话或定时调用。
          """
          llm = llm or self._llm
          results: dict[str, int] = {}

          # Episodic: 生成旧 episode 摘要
          results["episodic"] = await self.episodic.consolidate(llm)

          # Semantic: 衰减低置信度事实
          results["semantic"] = await self.semantic.consolidate(llm)

          # Vector: 清理旧条目
          if self.vector:
              results["vector"] = await self.vector.consolidate(llm)

          logger.info("Memory consolidation: %s", results)
          return results

      # ==================================================================
      # 工具方法
      # ==================================================================

      def get_working_messages(self) -> list[BaseMessage]:
          """获取 Working Memory 的原始消息列表（直接传给 LLM）。"""
          return self.working.get_messages()

      def get_working_summary(self) -> str:
          return self.working.summary

      async def get_entity_profile(self, entity_name: str | None = None) ->
  list[MemoryItem]:
          """获取实体画像——该实体的所有已知事实。"""
          return await self.semantic.retrieve_entity_facts(entity_name or
  self.entity_name)

      # ==================================================================
      # 内部
      # ==================================================================

      async def _extract_facts(self, user_message: str, assistant_message: str)
  -> None:
          """LLM 从一轮对话中提取结构化事实。"""
          if self._llm is None:
              return

          prompt = _FACT_EXTRACTION_PROMPT.format(
              entity_name=self.entity_name,
              conversation=f"用户: {user_message}\nAI: {assistant_message}",
          )

          try:
              from langchain_core.messages import HumanMessage
              response = await self._llm.ainvoke([HumanMessage(content=prompt)])
              raw = response.content if hasattr(response, "content") else
  str(response)
              facts = self._parse_facts(raw)
          except Exception as exc:
              logger.warning("Fact extraction failed: %s", exc)
              return

          if not facts:
              return

          items = [
              MemoryItem(
                  id=f"fact_{self.session_id}_{self.turn_count}_{i}",
                  content=f"{f['key']}: {f['value']}",
                  memory_type="semantic",
                  importance=f.get("confidence", 0.9),
                  metadata={
                      "entity_name": self.entity_name,
                      "key": f["key"],
                      "value": f["value"],
                      "confidence": f.get("confidence", 0.9),
                      "source_session": self.session_id,
                      "source_turn": self.turn_count,
                      "tags": f.get("tags", []),
                  },
              )
              for i, f in enumerate(facts)
          ]
          await self.semantic.store(items)
          logger.info("Extracted %d fact(s) for '%s'", len(items),
  self.entity_name)

      @staticmethod
      def _estimate_importance(user: str, assistant: str) -> float:
          """启发式估计对话重要性。"""
          # 简单规则：含关键信息词的对话更重要
          important_keywords = [
              "记住", "我叫", "我是", "我喜欢", "我住在", "我的电话",
              "偏好", "总是", "从不", "重要", "过敏", "紧急",
              "remember", "my name", "preference", "important",
          ]
          combined = (user + " " + assistant).lower()
          hits = sum(1 for kw in important_keywords if kw.lower() in combined)
          return min(0.9, 0.3 + hits * 0.15)

      @staticmethod
      def _parse_facts(raw: str) -> list[dict]:
          import json
          raw = raw.strip()
          if raw.startswith("```"):
              lines = raw.split("\n")
              raw = "\n".join(lines[1:]) if len(lines) > 1 else raw
              if raw.endswith("```"):
                  raw = raw[:-3]
          brace = raw.find("{")
          if brace == -1:
              return []
          try:
              data = json.loads(raw[brace:])
              return data.get("facts", [])
          except json.JSONDecodeError:
              return []

      @staticmethod
      async def _empty_result() -> list[MemoryItem]:
          return []


  # ============================================================================
  # 事实提取 Prompt
  # ============================================================================

  _FACT_EXTRACTION_PROMPT = """\
  从以下对话中提取关于 "{entity_name}" 的结构化信息。

  规则：
  - 只提取明确陈述或用户明确要求记住的事实
  - 每条事实用 key/value/confidence/tags 表示
  - key 用英文 snake_case
  - value 保留原始语言
  - confidence 0.0-1.0 (0.9=明确陈述, 0.5=暗示)
  - tags 用于分类：["personal", "preference", "health", "skill", "contact", 
  "goal"]
  - 无新事实时返回空数组

  对话:
  {conversation}

  仅返回 JSON:
  {{"facts": [{{"key": "...", "value": "...", "confidence": 0.9, "tags": 
  ["..."]}}]}}"""

  ---
  五、Prompt 注入策略

  5.1 注入位置与优先级

  Full prompt assembly for LLM call:

  ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │ System Message                                       │
  │                                                      │
  │ ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────┐ │
  │ │ 1. 人格 Skill (haven.md)             最高优先级  │ │
  │ │    "你是健健，灯塔医疗助手..."                   │ │
  │ └─────────────────────────────────────────────────┘ │
  │ ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────┐ │
  │ │ 2. 领域 Skill (coder.md, etc.)      Planner 激活 │ │
  │ │    "你是高级软件工程师..."                       │ │
  │ └─────────────────────────────────────────────────┘ │
  │ ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────┐ │
  │ │ 3. Semantic Memory                  结构化事实   │ │
  │ │    "[长期记忆] name: 张三 / job: 工程师 ..."    │ │
  │ └─────────────────────────────────────────────────┘ │
  │ ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────┐ │
  │ │ 4. Working Summary                超出窗口摘要   │ │
  │ │    "[近期对话摘要] 用户之前询问了..."            │ │
  │ └─────────────────────────────────────────────────┘ │
  │ ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────┐ │
  │ │ 5. Episodic Memory                 相关历史     │ │
  │ │    "[相关历史] [2026-05-20] 用户问了..."        │ │
  │ └─────────────────────────────────────────────────┘ │
  │ ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────┐ │
  │ │ 6. Vector Memory                   语义相关     │ │
  │ │    "[相关知识] ...相似内容..."                   │ │
  │ └─────────────────────────────────────────────────┘ │
  ├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ History Messages (Working Memory sliding window)     │
  │ ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────┐ │
  │ │ HumanMessage: "之前的消息..."                    │ │
  │ │ AIMessage: "之前的回复..."                       │ │
  │ │ HumanMessage: "当前问题"                         │ │
  │ └─────────────────────────────────────────────────┘ │
  └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

  注入优先级: 1 > 2 > 3 > 4 > 5 > 6
  Token 预算不足时的裁剪顺序: 6 先裁 → 5 → 4 → 3（事实不动）

  5.2 PromptBuilder 集成

  # src/haven/core/prompt.py — Memory V2 适配

  class PromptBuilder:
      """组装 system prompt + memory context。控制 token 预算。"""

      def __init__(self, max_system_tokens: int = 4000):
          self.max_system_tokens = max_system_tokens

      async def build(
          self,
          personality_skills: list[BaseSkill],
          domain_skills: list[BaseSkill],
          memory_context: MemoryContext,
          *,
          rag_context: str = "",
          task: str = "",
      ) -> str:
          """按优先级组装 system prompt，超出预算时从低优先级裁剪。"""

          sections: list[tuple[int, str]] = []  # (priority, text)

          # 1. 人格 skill（最高优先级，不参与裁剪）
          for skill in personality_skills:
              sections.append((0, skill.prompt_extension))

          # 2. 领域 skill
          for skill in domain_skills:
              sections.append((1, skill.prompt_extension))

          # 3. Semantic memory
          if memory_context.semantic:
              sections.append((2, self._format_semantic(memory_context)))

          # 4. Working summary
          # (由调用方注入，MemoryManager.get_working_summary())
          sections.append((3, ""))  # placeholder

          # 5. Episodic
          if memory_context.episodic:
              sections.append((4, self._format_episodic(memory_context)))

          # 6. Vector
          if memory_context.vector:
              sections.append((5, self._format_vector(memory_context)))

          # 7. RAG
          if rag_context:
              sections.append((6, rag_context))

          # 排序并拼接
          sections.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
          return self._fit_budget(sections)

  ---
  六、完整数据流

                       User: "帮我写一个 Python 爬虫"
                                │
          ┌─────────────────────▼─────────────────────────┐
          │ MemoryManager.retrieve(task="写Python爬虫")      │
          │                                                │
          │  ┌──────────┐  ┌──────────┐  ┌──────────┐  ┌──│──────┐
          │  │ Working  │  │ Episodic │  │ Semantic │  │Vector│   │
          │  │          │  │          │  │          │  │      │   │
          │  │ 最近20条 │  │ 搜索历史  │  │ 用户事实  │  │语义  │   │
          │  │ 消息     │  │ "爬虫"   │  │ name:张三│  │搜索  │   │
          │  │          │  │ 相关对话  │  │ job:工程师│  │"爬虫 │   │
          │  │          │  │ 3条匹配  │  │ prefer:  │  │技巧" │   │
          │  │          │  │          │  │  Python  │  │2条   │   │
          │  └────┬─────┘  └────┬─────┘  └────┬─────┘  └──│──────┘
          │       │             │             │           │
          │       └─────────┬───┴─────────────┴───────────┘
          │                 │
          │          MemoryContext
          │          ├─ working:   [20 items]
          │          ├─ semantic:  [name, job, preference]

          └─────────────────┬───────────────────────────┘
                            │
          ┌─────────────────▼───────────────────────────┐
          │ PromptBuilder.build(memory_context)          │
          │                                              │
          │ SystemMessage:                               │
          │   1. 人格 skill (haven.md)                   │
          │   2. 领域 skill (coder.md) ← Planner 激活    │
          │   3. [长期记忆] name: 张三, job: 工程师,     │
          │      preference: Python                      │
          │   4. [相关历史] [05-20] 用户曾问过爬虫问题.. │
          │   5. [相关知识] 使用 requests+BS4 解析HTML.. │
          │                                              │
          │ + History (最近20条消息)                      │
          │ + HumanMessage("帮我写一个 Python 爬虫")     │
          └─────────────────┬───────────────────────────┘
                            │
                      Runtime.run()

❯ 目标：                                                                        
                                                                                
  重构 Tool 系统。                                                              
                                                                                
  当前：                                                                        
                                                                                
  ToolRegistry                                                                  
  MCPManager                                                                    
                                                                                
  目标：                                                                        
                                                                                
  Provider Architecture                                                         
                                                                                
  providers/                                                                    
  ├── builtin                                                                   
  ├── mcp                                                                       
  ├── openapi                                                                   
  ├── custom                                                                    
                                                                                
  要求：                                                                        
                                                                                
  设计：                                                                        
                                                                                
  1. ToolProvider                                                               
  2. MCPProvider                                                                
  3. BuiltinProvider                                                            
  4. ToolManager                                                                
                                                                                
  统一工具发现机制。                                                            
                                                                                
  输出设计方案。                                                                
                                                                                
  不要修改代码。                                                                
                                                                                

● Here is the complete Provider Architecture design.

  ---
  Haven Tool 系统重构：Provider Architecture

  一、当前 vs 目标

  当前                                      目标
  ──────                                   ──────

  ToolRegistry (类级 dict)                 ToolManager
    register_tool(name, callable)            ├── BuiltinProvider
    register_lc_tool(BaseTool)               │     └── code_exec, file_ops,
  web_search...
  MCPManager                                │
    start() → 连接 → load_mcp_tools          ├── MCPProvider (per server)
    stop()                                   │     ├── filesystem_mcp
                                             │     └── web_fetch_mcp
  问题:                                      │
  · 双重注册 (register_tool + lc_tool)       ├── OpenAPIProvider
  · 无统一发现机制                            │     └── parse OpenAPI spec →
  tools
  · MCP 工具与内置工具分开管理                 │
  · 无工具来源概念                             └── CustomProvider
  · 无健康检查/重连                                 └── user-defined tools
  · 类级状态不线程安全
                                            统一接口: ToolProvider
                                            统一工具: BaseTool
                                            统一发现: provider.discover()

  ---
  二、目录结构

  src/haven/tools/
  ├── __init__.py                  # 统一导出
  │
  ├── base.py                      # HavenTool 基类 + ToolMetadata
  ├── manager.py                   # ToolManager — 编排所有 provider
  │
  ├── providers/                   # 提供者层
  │   ├── __init__.py
